# P9.3 reconciliation: J0 (shared prerequisites and testkit)

Task P9.3, jackin lane. This file records steps J0a and J0b of the approved plan (`/private/tmp/claude-501/plans/P9.3-design-v6.md` section 14 and 15.2, `/private/tmp/claude-501/plans/P9.3-test-plan-v6.md` section 1.3 for the TK items and section 8 for the J0 rows). Base: `dbb35f2f7` on `wt/P9.3`, rebased on `origin/feature/openshell-native`. The console crate (`jackin-tui`) and the entry are J1 and are not touched. The gate is `mise exec -- mbx +1.99.1 xtask ci --fast`.

## J0a: what changed

| Item | Change | Where |
|---|---|---|
| P-1 | `SandboxView.created_at: Option<Timestamp>` (the gateway `ObjectMeta.created_time`, in seconds) and `SandboxSummary.created_at`; `Unexported.uncommitted` is a count and `Unexported::unpushed()` counts unexported refs; `FakeGateway` stamps `created_at`; contract check `created_at_is_reported_and_stable` | `jackin-openshell`, `jackin-core`, `jackin-session`, `jackin-testkit` |
| P-2 | `plan_account_edits` and `AccountPlan::apply` (one account add, remove and credential-change sequence), `build_for_workspace`, `lookup_workspace`; `jackin account add\|rm`, `jackin build` and `jackin load` call them | `jackin-session`, `jackin` |
| P-3 | `Display` for `Percent` and `UnavailableReason`; `LimitWindow::{used_text, remaining_text, reset_text}`; `jackin usage` prints through them | `jackin-usage`, `jackin` |
| P-4 | `Clock::utc_offset_secs`; `SystemClock` reads `chrono::Local` (`chrono` with the `clock` feature, already in the lock file); `FakeClock::set_utc_offset` | `jackin-core`, `jackin-session`, `jackin-testkit` |
| P-5 | `Session::changes(sandbox, cancel) -> ChangeSet` with files, statuses and hunks | `jackin-session` |
| P-6 | `CheckId::{ALL, label, fix}`; `jackin doctor` appends `  fix: ...` to `warn` and `fail` lines | `jackin-session`, `jackin` |
| P-7 | `LaunchFailure::{summary, next_step}`, `LaunchError::text`, `error_chain`, `main_process_ended`, `sandbox_missing`, `env_file_problem`, `hook_failure`; `crates/jackin/src/report.rs` keeps only command-specific wording | `jackin-session`, `jackin` |
| P-8 | `ConfigEdit::SetAccountCredential` | `jackin-core` |
| P-11 | `Session::project_origin` (D08 host git runner, `origin` only, exact `github.com` host) | `jackin-session` |
| P-10, TK-2 | `SandboxHandle::shells()` returns a `ShellWitness` per shell terminal: workdir, env, raw input, size history, closed flag | `jackin-testkit` |
| TK-3 | `SandboxHandle::resizes()`: every `Resize` the transport received, without the attach size | `jackin-testkit` |
| TK-4 | `SandboxHandle::set_replay_window` (the replay can start inside an escape sequence); the 20-attachment cap (`ResourceExhausted`) and `FakeGateway::set_attachment_cap` | `jackin-testkit` |
| TK-5 | `ScriptedConnector`, a `GatewayConnector` with one failure script per gateway; the trait and `ConnectError` moved from `jackin` to `jackin-session` so the testkit can implement it | `jackin-testkit`, `jackin-session`, `jackin` |
| TK-6 | `Fault::HoldAt { trigger }`, `FakeGateway::{release, held, until_held}`: a call that matches a trigger waits until it is released or its cancel token fires | `jackin-testkit` |
| TK-7 | the fake honours `RepaintPolicy::NudgeAfter`; it fires on `SandboxHandle::advance_repaint()` only. `jackin_openshell::contract::NUDGE_CASES` is the shared oracle; `GrpcGateway` runs it with paused tokio time and `advance` | `jackin-testkit`, `jackin-openshell` |
| TK-8 | `SandboxHandle::raw_input()`: the bytes an `Owner` attachment sent, before the fake supervisor swallows `0x04` or `0x10 0x11` | `jackin-testkit` |
| TK-9 | `SandboxHandle::set_stream_exit_status`: the `Exited` event of `exit_main` carries another status than the phase code (255 versus 1) | `jackin-testkit` |

`ConsoleRig`, `click`, `status_is_neutral` and `reasons()` (TK-1) belong to J1 and are not built here.

## Decisions the plan did not state, and where the plan does not fit the code

1. **Codex accounts without a ChatGPT login (plan 7.3, "add runs `Session::sync_account` first").** `sync_account` returns `AccountError::Unsupported` for a Codex account whose source is not a ChatGPT login, and `jackin account add codex --credential env:X` failed on it. The plan and D28 also need a usage-only Codex account with an `op://` source (the account form, T14, T30). One shared sequence cannot do both, so the plan syncs only accounts whose source is a ChatGPT login (the only ones that have an OpenShell provider). Every other account is stored without a provider. This changes the CLI: `jackin account add codex --credential env:X` now succeeds. The CLI test that expected the failure now expects the stored account and an untouched gateway (`a_codex_account_without_a_chatgpt_login_is_stored_for_usage_and_never_reaches_the_gateway`). `sync_account` and the launch path are unchanged, so a launch with such an account is still a typed `Unsupported`.
2. **"File counts in `Unexported`" (P-1).** The plan names no type. `uncommitted` counts the entries of `git status --porcelain --untracked-files=all` in every checkout plus the entries of `git stash list`. `unpushed()` counts branches, worktrees and a detached head that the host lacks. `Display` keeps the text `uncommitted changes`, so the CLI wording and its tests are unchanged. Commit counts would need the host history in the sandbox and are not provided.
3. **`GatewayConnector` and `ConnectError` moved to `jackin-session`.** TK-5 puts a scripted connector in the testkit, and the testkit cannot depend on `jackin`. The trait only uses core and openshell types. J1's `SessionConnector` can reuse it.
4. **Build is a free function.** The plan calls it "the build-for-workspace function". `jackin build` must not connect to a gateway (its test `build_prints_the_final_image_id_caches_it_and_needs_no_gateway`), so `build_for_workspace(engine, catalog, config, request, progress, cancel)` takes no `Session`. For the same reason `AccountPlan::apply` takes `Option<&Session>`: `jackin account add claude` still never connects.
5. **The fake nudge is a trigger, not tokio time.** The fake has no timer: `advance_repaint()` fires the nudge, as the plan says. The real transport already uses `tokio::time::Instant`; the new real test pauses tokio time and calls `advance`. Both read one table (`NUDGE_CASES`), so a change of the expected window list turns both red.
6. **Raw log and viewers.** `GrpcGateway` never sends the input of a `Viewer` attachment, so the fake does not log it. Bytes of a second `Owner` attachment that holds no lease are logged, because the real transport sends them and the supervisor drops them.
7. **Attachment cap.** OpenShell `MAX_CONNECTIONS_PER_SANDBOX` is 20 and is shared by SSH sessions and service forwards (`crates/openshell-server/src/grpc/sandbox.rs:2945` at v0.1.6). The fake counts main-process attachments only, and shell terminals (`ExecSandboxInteractive`) do not count.
8. **`Session::changes` returns files and hunks only.** The baseline `ChangeSet` also carries `unpushed`; the console takes that from `Unexported::unpushed()` through `work_state`, which needs the config.
9. **Output changes of existing commands.** `jackin ls --json` and `jackin inspect --json` print `created_at` in each summary when the gateway recorded it (additive). `jackin doctor` appends the fix text to `warn` and `fail` lines. `jackin usage` text is unchanged. The existing key-set tests for `ls` and `inspect` pin the new key.
10. **Repository rules met.** `rewrite/DESIGN.md` is not edited (only the orchestrator edits it); the D11 and D15 text of P-9 is for the orchestrator. `xtask containers` rejects a computed `Command::new`, so the TZ probe of the P-4 test runs through `/bin/sh -c 'exec "$0" ...'`. `cargo shear` reported `tests/common/*.rs` and `tests/support/trap.rs` as unlinked after I added four top-level `tests/*.rs` files to `jackin-session`; the same tests under one directory binary, `tests/seams/`, cleared it (the cause was not investigated).

## J0a: new tests, the named break of each, red and green

Method: each break was applied to the committed tree by a script (`red.py` in the task scratchpad), the narrowest `nextest` filter for the test was run, the file was restored, and the same filter ran again. "Red" is the first failing assertion; `actual` is the left side of `assert_eq!` and `expected` the right side. A long value is cut at the first difference. Rows count the tests of the filter, not of the crate. Run one by hand with, for example, `mise exec -- mbx +1.99.1 nextest run -p jackin-session --all-features -E 'binary(seams) and test(/origin::/)'`.

Existing tests that changed with the code and have no new break: the key-set tests for `ls` and `inspect` (`created_at`), `Unexported { uncommitted: n }` literals in the session export and plan tests, the CLI account test (decision 1), `crates/jackin-core/tests/views.rs` (an unstamped summary omits `created_at`), the `Clock` impls of the core tests (`utc_offset_secs` returns 0).

## J0b: what changed (P-9)

| Item | Change | Where |
|---|---|---|
| Config fields | `[env]`; `[workspaces.<n>.env]`; `[workspaces.<n>.role_env.<r>]`; `[workspaces.<n>] roles = [...]`. `Workspace` gains `roles: AllowedRoles`, `env: EnvTable`, `role_env`; `Config::env()`; `Workspace::new(path, docker)` | `jackin-core` |
| Types | `PlainEnvValue` (refuses `op://` at the start, a newline and NUL; `EnvValueError`), `RoleSet` (never empty), `AllowedRoles::{All, Only}`, `EnvScope::{Global, Workspace, WorkspaceRole}`, `EnvTable` | `jackin-core` |
| Edits | `ConfigEdit::SetEnv { scope, name, value: Option<PlainEnvValue> }` (`None` unsets), `ConfigEdit::SetWorkspaceRoles { workspace, roles }` | `jackin-core` |
| Parse errors | an `op://` or non-text value, a name outside the variable alphabet, a reserved name (`EnvName::is_reserved`, the core authority), an empty or malformed `roles`, a `roles` or `role_env` role that is not configured, a `default_role` outside `roles` | `jackin-core` |
| Plan | `resolve_env(&EnvLayers, &RoleManifest, &answers, Prompting)` is public and `plan_launch` calls it; `PlanError::RoleNotAllowed { workspace, role }` | `jackin-session` |

## J0b: decisions the plan did not state

1. **No new `ConfigError` variant.** A reserved name and a default role outside the allowed roles use `ConfigError::InvalidValue` with the keys `env.<NAME>` and `workspaces.<w>.default_role`. The exhaustive `label` matches in the core tests did not change. The text for a reserved name reads `invalid value for env.DOCKER_HOST: reserved by jackin`.
2. **`SetEnv.value` is `Option<PlainEnvValue>`.** Design 4.4 moves a variable with "unset at the old scope, set at the new", so an edit must be able to unset. An emptied `role_env.<r>` table is dropped, so a render has no empty table.
3. **Role references.** A role named in `roles` or `role_env` must be a configured role, as `default_role` already must. `RemoveRole` refuses a role that an allow-list or a `role_env` table still names (`ConfigReference::{WorkspaceRoles, WorkspaceRoleEnv}`), which is what T17 asks for the allow-list. The console editor's "a name in the list that is no longer a configured role" is a stale in-memory state, not a config state.
4. **`SetWorkspaceDefaultRole` and `SetWorkspaceRoles` keep the default inside the allowed roles.** Setting a default outside the set, or narrowing the set so it leaves out the default, is refused. The console clears the default first (its rule "unchecking the default clears it").
5. **`resolve_env` and the questions.** A scoped value for a declared name means the manifest entry is not asked, also with `Prompting::Interactive`. A declared name with no answer and no scoped value behaves as before (default with `Never`, a question otherwise). The Docker variables still override (`values.extend(docker.environment())`). `LaunchRequest::env` keeps its meaning (answers); `EnvUndeclared` is for answers only.
6. **`RoleNotAllowed` comes before the account is selected,** so a refused launch reads the same whatever the accounts are.
7. **`Workspace::new`.** `Workspace` has public fields, so every literal needed the three new fields. `Workspace::new(path, docker)` builds the empty case; the CLI `workspace add`, the ad hoc workspace of `lookup_workspace` and the core tests use it.
8. **DESIGN D11 and D15 text** (P-9 in plan section 14) is not edited: only the orchestrator edits `rewrite/DESIGN.md`. API.md section 1 and 4 carry the config and plan changes.

## J0b: new tests, the named break of each, red and green

Tests: `crates/jackin-core/tests/config_schema.rs` (the full file gains the three env scopes and `roles`; `env_at_three_scopes_and_the_allowed_roles_parse_into_the_config`; 13 invalid-value rows and 3 cross-reference rows), `crates/jackin-core/tests/config_edit.rs` (5 edit rows, 8 rejected rows, `env_edits_replace_unset_and_drop_an_emptied_role_table`, `moving_a_variable_between_scopes_is_an_unset_and_a_set_in_one_edit_list`, `a_role_that_a_workspace_allows_or_gives_variables_cannot_be_removed`, `the_default_role_must_stay_inside_the_allowed_roles`, `a_plain_env_value_refuses_a_1password_reference_and_control_characters`), and `crates/jackin-session/tests/plan/{scoped_env,allowed_roles}.rs`. The reserved-name breaks are two: one neutralizes `reserved_env_name` (shared by the config check and `SetEnv`), one removes the parser's own check, which is what puts the workspace key in the message (`workspaces.w.env.X` and not `env.X`).

### P-9

- **Break:** EnvLayers::of: the workspace and role scope is ignored
  - Red: 7 tests run: 6 passed, 1 failed, 37 skipped; failing: `jackin-session::plan scoped_env::the_launch_plan_reads_the_three_scopes_of_the_config_into_the_env_file`

## J0 review fixes (code review, security review, red/green report)

Inputs: `/private/tmp/claude-501/plans/P9.3-J0-review.md`, `P9.3-J0-security.md`, `P9.3-J0-redgreen.md`. Base for the fixes: `b1d5664eb`. The tests for these fixes are in the section "J0 fix round: tests" below. Each fix below was run once against a throwaway probe (the reviewers' probes plus two of mine, none committed) and the existing suites stay green.

| Finding | Resolution |
|---|---|
| CR1 high, stash count read the reflog | `Remote::uncommitted` asks `git rev-parse --verify --quiet refs/stash` and counts `max(stash list lines, 1)` when the ref exists. A stash with an expired reflog is `Unexported { uncommitted: 1 }`. The reviewer probe is green. |
| CR2, orphan provider for some edit lists | `plan_account_edits` applies the list to a copy and compares each touched account before and after (`has_provider`). Forget when it had a provider and has none; sync when it has one now. Order no longer matters. The reviewers' two probes are green. |
| CR3, `build_for_workspace` dropped the workspace Docker flag | `build_for_workspace` passes the looked-up workspace to `plan_image`, the function that owns the image inputs, so `jackin build` and `jackin load` cache the same tag. This reverses the P5.4 rule that `jackin build` ignores the workspace flag (P5.4 row J2). The test that pinned the old rule is inverted (see below). |
| CR4 doctor git fix | `Check` carries `fix`; a git that is too old reads `Upgrade git to the required version`, a missing or unusable git keeps `CheckId::fix`. `jackin doctor` prints `Check.fix`. |
| CR5 `SetEnv` reserved-name key | `EnvScope::key` builds the key of the scope (`env.X`, `workspaces.w.env.X`, `workspaces.w.role_env.r.X`); one conversion to `ScopedEnvName` in `set_env`. |
| CR6 only-role fallback ignored `roles` | `RoleCatalog::load` takes the only role among the roles the workspace allows. |
| CR7 provider encoded four times | `Account::chatgpt_login` is the one place that matches `CodexLogin`; `has_provider`, `codex_auth_mode`, `plan_launch`, `sync_locked`, `forget_account` and the account plan use it. |
| CR8 `Option<&Session>` plus `GatewayRequired` | `AccountPlan` is `Offline(OfflinePlan) | Gateway(GatewayPlan)`. `OfflinePlan::apply` takes no session; `GatewayPlan::apply` and `AccountPlan::apply` take `&Session`. `AccountEditError::GatewayRequired` and its CLI mapping are deleted. The CLI connects only for `Gateway`. |
| CR9 stale API.md | `work_state` text, the section 8 connector block, and the `plan_launch` step order are corrected. |
| CR10 nits | `serde(default)` removed from `created_at`; `RoleSet::len` and `is_empty` removed (unused). `offset_probe` was a helper that ran as a test; it is deleted in the fix round (row CK-1). |
| CR question 1 | Verified: one untracked file over 16 MiB made `read_files` fail with `GatewayError::OutputTooLarge`, so `Session::changes` failed as a whole (probe: 17 MiB file, `changes` is now `Ok`). Each untracked file is read with `head -c 262145` in one program run; over 256 KiB is `ChangedFile.unread = Some(Unread::TooLarge)`, a link or other non-regular file is `Some(Unread::NotRegular)`. Untracked symlinks are no longer followed. |
| CR question 1b, 500-file cap | `ChangeSet.omitted` counts the untracked files beyond the 500 listed. Design v6 gives the console no rule for truncation, and a silent cut is a wrong state, so the count is returned and the console decides the wording. |
| CR question 2 | Intended and kept: `--untracked-files=all` is a count of entries; a project with hundreds of thousands of untracked files makes `work_state` `Unknown(InspectionFailed)`, which blocks a guarded purge. That is the safe side. |
| CR question 3 | `git diff HEAD` did not rewrite the index after a touched file (probe, git 2.54.0), while plain `git diff` did and `git --no-optional-locks diff` did not. Every sandbox git call now runs with `--no-optional-locks` (one place, `Remote::git`). The probe was wrong for a touched file: `git diff HEAD` still rewrote the index; see "CH-2 fix: the changes half". |
| SEC F1 | `PlainEnvValue::parse` trims leading whitespace and U+FEFF and refuses a value that starts with `op://` or `openshell:resolve:`, matched without regard to letter case (`EnvValueError::OnePassword`, `Placeholder`). No name rule: design 4.4 keeps names such as `API_KEY` allowed with a note, and jackin-start's name rule (`kinds`) only classifies. Values such as `ghp_x`, `sk-...`, `"op://x"` (quoted) and `see op://x` stay allowed. |
| SEC F2 | `project_origin` runs `git config --get-all remote.origin.url` (more than one value gives `None`), then `git ls-remote --get-url origin`, which applies `insteadOf` and takes the first URL. |
| SEC F3 | `RoleCatalog::load` refuses a role outside the workspace `roles` (`RoleError::NotAllowed`) before the git checkout. `build_for_workspace` and `jackin load` pass through it. `plan_launch` keeps its own `RoleNotAllowed` check, before account selection, because a `LoadedRole` from `load_path` carries no proof; it is the typed backstop of design 4.5. |
| SEC F4 | `EnvTable` is keyed by `ScopedEnvName`, an `EnvName` that is not reserved. `EnvLayers` and `resolve_env` cannot hold a reserved name. The four `reserved_env_name` calls are gone: the parser, `SetEnv` and `Config::check` loops were the same rule four times. |
| RG P-9 order | `plan_launch` checks `RoleNotAllowed` before `select_account` (unchanged); the survivor was a missing test row (addendum row P9-1). |
| RG P-9 `check_workspace` backstop | Subsumed by F4: a reserved name in `Workspace.env` or `role_env` cannot be built, so `AddWorkspace` cannot carry one and the recheck loops in `check_workspace` and `check_references` are deleted. The type is the backstop. The addendum replaces the planned `AddWorkspace` row with a `ScopedEnvName::new` row and keeps the parse and `SetEnv` rows. |
| RG TK-7, gaps | Test-only; in the addendum. |

### Tests touched in this round (mechanical or forced)

- `crates/jackin-session/tests/accounts/edit.rs`: `run` passes `&world.session`; the offline test destructures `AccountPlan::Offline`; `a_plan_that_changes_a_provider_refuses_to_run_without_a_gateway` is deleted, because the state it refuses can no longer be built.
- `crates/jackin-session/tests/plan/scoped_env.rs`: `table` builds `ScopedEnvName` keys.
- `crates/jackin-session/tests/export/changes.rs`: the struct literals gain `unread: None` and `omitted: 0`.
- `crates/jackin-core/tests/config_edit.rs`: the row `unsetting a reserved variable` (workspace scope) expected the global key; it now expects `invalid:workspaces.proj.env.JACKIN_LAUNCH_ID` (CR5, not mechanical: the old row pinned the defect).
- `crates/jackin/tests/cli/build.rs`: the test that pinned "a workspace Docker flag does not change the image build makes" was inverted here and is now deleted; row BD-1 below replaces it with a test of the effect (a load after a build makes no new image).

## J0 fix round: tests, the named break of each, red and green

Plan: `/private/tmp/claude-501/plans/P9.3-J0-fix-test-plan.md`. Verdict, approved with amendments: `/private/tmp/claude-501/plans/P9.3-J0-fix-test-plan-review.md`. The rows below follow the plan as the verdict amended it.

Method: each break is one edit to production code, applied to the committed tree by a script (`red.py` in the task scratchpad) that checks the old text occurs once, runs the narrowest filter, restores the file byte for byte, and runs the same filter again. The command is `mise exec -- mbx +1.99.1 nextest run -p <crate> --all-features --no-fail-fast -E '<filter>'`. All 52 breaks compile clean (the workspace denies unused code), all 52 are red on an assertion (exit 100, never a compile error or a hang), and all 52 are green after the revert. `git status` after the run shows no production file changed. "Red" is the first failing assertion, left side first; a long value is cut at 170 characters.

Decisions the plan and the brief did not state:

- CH-1. The fake gateway had no output limit, so the 17 MiB case could not be red. `FakeGateway::run_program` now fails a stdout or stderr over 16 MiB, and `read_files` a file over 16 MiB, with `GatewayError::OutputTooLarge`. The Core contract gains `output_over_the_limit_is_an_error` (`CORE_CHECKS` has 18 names). It pins an output and a file of exactly 16 MiB (returned whole) and one byte more (`OutputTooLarge`, typed, with the operation and the limit), and it runs on the fake (`jackin-testkit` contract test) and on the real transport (`jackin-openshell` contract test, in-process server). The live e2e run uses the same check against a real gateway. The check writes its test file through a positional argument, because the in-process server rewrites only an argument that starts with `/sandbox`.
- Reserved names. `OPENAI_API_KEY` and `CODEX_HOME` were already reserved, by the prefixes `OPENAI_` and `CODEX_` of `RESERVED_PREFIXES` (`ids.rs:21`); the agent-spec branch of `EnvName::is_reserved` overlaps them. No code change. The EN-1 test pins both names, and the breaks below show that each is red only when its prefix is removed (and, for `CODEX_HOME`, the agent branch too). `ScopedEnvName::new` is the only constructor: `grep -rn "ScopedEnvName(" crates` finds only the struct definition and the one `Ok(ScopedEnvName(name))` in `new` (`config.rs:112, 123`); the type derives `Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash` and has no `Default`, `Deserialize` or `From`. The callers of `new` are `config/edit.rs:347`, `config/parse.rs:359` and the test `plan/scoped_env.rs:17`.
- AC-1. The table is every ordered pair of five edits (`AddAccount` with a login, `AddAccount` with a key, `RemoveAccount`, `SetAccountCredential` to a login, `SetAccountCredential` to a file) on three targets (an existing login account, an existing file account, an absent name): 75 pairs, 22 plannable. The oracle is the loaded final config: the gateway holds a provider for exactly the accounts whose credential is a ChatGPT login. The 53 refused pairs are asserted to leave the config and the providers as they were. The pair count is asserted (22), so a change of the edit rules shows up as a failed count and not as a silently smaller table.
- CK-1. One test, re-run under `TZ`; the child runs the same test with `JACKIN_CLOCK_CHILD` set, prints the offset and returns. `offset_probe` is deleted. No manifest change. The `TZ` forms `XXX-5:30`, `XXX5` and `UTC` were run on macOS only; Linux was not run.
- TK7-1. The shared oracle `NUDGE_CASES` stays. The delay is 2 s of paused tokio time. The test pauses, lets the pump start (`settled`), advances the measured quiet time (`delay` minus the time since the session opened minus 20 ms) and asserts no window change, advances the rest of the delay plus 10 ms and waits for the windows in a `spawn_blocking` poll with a `std::time::Instant` deadline (so the paused clock cannot advance on its own), then advances three delays and asserts the nudge fired once.
- BD-1. The session test builds the image for each of the four (role, workspace) Docker pairs, then runs `ensure_image` on the image request of `plan_launch` for the same workspace against the same fake engine, and asserts the image is a cache hit with the same tag and id and that the engine's build list did not grow. It also asserts that the tags of the two workspace settings differ when the role has Docker off and are equal when the role has it on, and that an unsaved directory builds the role-only image. The CLI test builds with `build --json` in a Docker workspace, runs `load`, and asserts `load` made no build.
- EV-1 and EN-1 through `SetEnv`. A `PlainEnvValue` that holds a refused value cannot be built, so `SetEnv` cannot carry one; the refusal rows go through `PlainEnvValue::parse` and `Config::parse` (which names the key). The reserved-name rows go through `ScopedEnvName::new`, `SetEnv` at the three scopes (set and unset), and `Config::parse` at the three scopes (including a quoted key).
- RC-1. The plan's second assertion, that the scripted runner saw no `git` run, is not possible: `TestRunner` runs git for real and records only scripted programs, so `seen()` is always empty and the assertion would be vacuous. The state assertion (the role cache directory is still empty) is the proof; it is red on the break (row RC-1, `left: 1, right: 0`).

Rows and parts not kept:

- CH-2, `changes`: kept after the fix below. It was held back on the fix commit because `git diff HEAD` rewrote the sandbox index even with `--no-optional-locks`; `Remote::changes` now runs `git diff-index -p --find-renames HEAD --` (section "CH-2 fix: the changes half").
- AC-3: no runtime row (compile-time claim, as approved).
- CH-1: the FIFO and the vanished file are not tested (approved: a FIFO is not listed by `git ls-files --others`, and a vanished file needs a race).
- The plan's `AddWorkspace` row: a reserved name cannot be put in a `Workspace`, so the edit cannot be built (approved).

Section 0 of the plan (tests touched in the fix commit):

- `jackin-core/tests/config_edit.rs`, row `unsetting a reserved variable` (expects `invalid:workspaces.proj.env.JACKIN_LAUNCH_ID`): red on the production source of `b1d5664eb` (a `git archive` copy in the scratchpad, with only this expected key changed): `crates/jackin-core/tests/config_edit.rs:851: assertion left == right failed: unsetting a reserved variable | left: "invalid:env.JACKIN_LAUNCH_ID" | right: "invalid:workspaces.proj.env.JACKIN_LAUNCH_ID"` (1 test run: 0 passed, 1 failed). Green on the fix commit. The break EN1-global-key below reproduces it in the current tree.
- The CLI build test is replaced (BD-1). The other four are mechanical.
- Survivor evidence for TK7-1: the nudge tests of `b1d5664eb..87934a8b4` (`the_repaint_nudge_resizes_one_row_less_and_back_after_the_delay` and `the_repaint_nudge_follows_tokio_time_for_every_shared_case`, before this round) were **green** with the real transport waiting 2x the delay (`main_pty.rs`: `Instant::now() + delay * 2`): `2 tests run: 2 passed, 84 skipped`, because `eventually` sleeps on the paused clock, which auto-advances. The new test is red on the same break (row TK7-1).

### ST-1

- **Test:** `jackin-session::export work_state::a_stash_counts_from_its_ref_so_an_expired_reflog_still_blocks_a_guarded_purge`
- **Break:** `Remote::uncommitted` counts `git stash list` lines and ignores `refs/stash`
  - Red: 1 test run: 0 passed, 1 failed, 63 skipped; failing: `jackin-session::export work_state::a_stash_counts_from_its_ref_so_an_expired_reflog_still_blocks_a_guarded_purge`
    - `crates/jackin-session/tests/export/work_state.rs:274: assertion `left == right` failed | left: Exported | right: Unexported(Unexported { uncommitted: 1, branches: [], detached: None, worktrees: [], unusable: [] })`
  - Green after revert: 1 test run: 1 passed, 63 skipped

### AC-1

### CH-2

- **Test:** `jackin-session::export changes::asking_for_the_work_state_never_rewrites_the_sandbox_index`
- **Break:** `Remote::git` drops `--no-optional-locks`
  - Red: 1 test run: 0 passed, 1 failed, 63 skipped; failing: `jackin-session::export changes::asking_for_the_work_state_never_rewrites_the_sandbox_index`
    - `crates/jackin-session/tests/export/changes.rs:294: assertion `left == right` failed | left: Sha256("b8ab89a7902cc64b48a2ec3f4914807f4934692cf3e6084097fa2b58638c0d33") | right: Sha256("cf5c5c633740fb35ca391aadce329018418f12b2a679aaae44c9f79c0e28a4a2")`
  - Green after revert: 1 test run: 1 passed, 63 skipped

### CH-2 fix: the changes half

`Remote::changes` ran `git diff HEAD`, which refreshes and rewrites the sandbox index when a tracked file has a changed mtime, even with `--no-optional-locks` (git 2.54.0). It now runs `git diff-index -p --find-renames HEAD --` with the same `-c core.quotepath=false --no-color --no-ext-diff --no-textconv -U3` flags. `diff-index` is plumbing: it reads the index and the tree and never refreshes or writes the index.

- **Test:** `jackin-session::export changes::asking_for_the_changes_never_rewrites_the_sandbox_index`. It sets a tracked file's mtime, snapshots the bytes of the sandbox `project/.git/index`, calls `Session::changes` (expecting an empty `ChangeSet`), asserts the bytes are unchanged, then runs `git status --porcelain` without the flag and asserts that the bytes do change (the instrument check). The work-state test shares the `touch_readme` and `index_digest` helpers.
- **Break:** `Remote::changes` goes back to `git diff` in place of `git diff-index -p`
  - Red: 7 tests run: 6 passed, 1 failed, 326 skipped; failing: `jackin-session::export changes::asking_for_the_changes_never_rewrites_the_sandbox_index`
    - `crates/jackin-session/tests/export/changes.rs:329: assertion `left == right` failed | left: Sha256("7f2faf6fc0147c7062dd1a96158f68a5ff7ebf7502917ebf5328bea63e6a2211") | right: Sha256("123a8b57b7d13b6ad8ced86f808f661fa9f0cce7bac35829d93e0332fb37e5dc")`
  - Green after revert: 7 tests run: 7 passed, 326 skipped (all `changes::` tests, including the rename test, the edit, delete and untracked listing, and the 500-file cap).
- **Output identity:** in a scratch repository with an edit, a deletion, a staged rename, a binary change, a mode change, a non-ASCII path, a touched file with unchanged content, a staged new file and an untracked file, `git --no-optional-locks diff ... HEAD --` and `git --no-optional-locks diff-index -p ... HEAD --` print byte-identical output (`cmp`, 53 lines, eight `diff --git` headers).
- **Audit of the sandbox git calls** (each run with `--no-optional-locks` on a repository whose two tracked files had a changed mtime; digest of `.git/index` before and after): `diff HEAD` changed it; `diff-index -p --find-renames HEAD --`, `status --porcelain --untracked-files=all`, `rev-parse --git-dir`, `rev-parse --verify --quiet HEAD`, `rev-parse --verify --quiet refs/stash`, `rev-parse --absolute-git-dir`, `stash list`, `worktree list --porcelain -z`, `for-each-ref`, `ls-files --others --exclude-standard -z` and `bundle create` left it unchanged. `diff HEAD` was the only porcelain that refreshes the index.
- **Git version:** `diff-index -p` and `--find-renames` are old plumbing options. The sandbox needs only the git of Ubuntu 24.04 (2.43.0, decision D08, live runs in `evidence/P5.1b-upload.md`); the role images build a later git, and the host floor is the per-line table of D08 and P5.3. The run above is git 2.54.0.

## J0 fix round: independent red/green review fixes

Input: `/private/tmp/claude-501/plans/P9.3-J0-fix-redgreen.md` (verdict CHANGES_REQUIRED on `af9e10cf2`). Method: each break is one edit applied by a script (`/private/tmp/claude-501/p93-j0fix2/red.py`) that checks the old text occurs once, runs the filter with `mise exec -- mbx +1.99.1 nextest run -p <crate> --all-features --no-fail-fast -E '<filter>'`, restores the file byte for byte, and runs the filter again. A break that does not compile (`last` unused in `error_chain`) was extended to remove the dead variable, so each red is an assertion.

### EC-1 (changed)

- **Test:** `jackin-session::launch failure_text::an_error_chain_prints_each_cause_once_and_drops_only_a_repeat_of_the_line_before`. The table gains `["a", "b", "b"]` giving `a: b` and `["ab", "b"]` giving `ab: b`. The existing code gives both outputs; the test is green unbroken (`1 test run: 1 passed, 332 skipped`).
- **Break:** `error_chain` drops a cause only when it equals the whole text so far (`line != text` in place of `line != last`)
  - Red: 1 test run: 0 passed, 1 failed, 332 skipped; failing: `jackin-session::launch failure_text::an_error_chain_prints_each_cause_once_and_drops_only_a_repeat_of_the_line_before`
    - `crates/jackin-session/tests/launch/failure_text.rs:165: assertion `left == right` failed: ["a", "b", "b"] | left: "a: b: b" | right: "a: b"`
  - Green after revert: 1 test run: 1 passed, 332 skipped
- **Break:** `error_chain` drops a cause when the text so far ends with it (`!text.ends_with(&line)`)
  - Red: 1 test run: 0 passed, 1 failed, 332 skipped; failing: the same test
    - `crates/jackin-session/tests/launch/failure_text.rs:165: assertion `left == right` failed: ["ab", "b"] | left: "ab" | right: "ab: b"`
  - Green after revert: 1 test run: 1 passed, 332 skipped

### TK7-1 (changed)

- **Test:** `jackin-openshell::gateway attach_ssh::the_repaint_nudge_follows_tokio_time_for_every_shared_case`. The test takes a `std::time::Instant` before `try_open`, and after `tokio::time::pause()` computes `quiet = delay - (opening.elapsed() + 20 ms)`. It advances `quiet`, asserts no window change, then advances `delay.saturating_sub(quiet) + 10 ms`. The old fixed half-delay step is gone, so "nothing fires early" holds to within the measured slack and not to 50% of the delay.
- **Break:** the real transport schedules the nudge at 0.75x the delay (`main_pty.rs:563`, `Instant::now() + delay * 3 / 4`)
  - Red: 2 tests run: 1 passed, 1 failed, 111 skipped; failing: `jackin-openshell::gateway attach_ssh::the_repaint_nudge_follows_tokio_time_for_every_shared_case`
    - `crates/jackin-openshell/tests/gateway/attach_ssh.rs:523: assertion `left == right` failed: the nudge shrinks one row and restores it at the attach size: the nudge came early | left: [(100, 29), (100, 30)] | right: []`
  - Green after revert: 2 tests run: 2 passed, 111 skipped
- **Determinism:** `--stress-count 30` of both nudge tests under 36 busy loops (twice the 18 CPUs; started with `sh -c 'while :; do :; done'`, killed by PID, `kill -0` found none alive; load average 41 to 72): `30/30 stress run iterations: 30 passed`, run again on the final test text (the unchecked subtraction was replaced by `saturating_sub` for clippy) with the same result, and a third time after merging `feature/openshell-native` (`30/30 stress run iterations: 30 passed`). Logs: `/private/tmp/claude-501/p93-j0fix2/stress.log`, `stress2.log`, `stress3.log`. All five breaks of this section were rerun on the merged tree and are red on the same lines and green after the revert.

### S1: the stderr half of the limit (contract check changed)

- **Test:** the Core contract check `output_over_the_limit_is_an_error` (`jackin-openshell/src/contract.rs`). It now also writes exactly 16 MiB to stderr (`yes | head -c N >&2`) and expects it returned whole, then writes one byte more and expects `OutputTooLarge` of `Program` on `Stderr` with the 16 MiB limit. `output_too_large` takes the stream. The check runs on both transports through `jackin-testkit::gateway contract::core_group_passes_on_the_fake_with_every_published_check_ok` and `jackin-openshell::gateway contract::core_group_passes_against_grpc_gateway_on_the_in_process_server`.

### S4: the agent-spec branch of `EnvName::is_reserved` (deleted)

The branch reserved `AgentSpec::reserved_env` (`CODEX_HOME`) and the four `CredentialKeys` names of each agent (`CODEX_AUTH_ACCESS_TOKEN`, `CODEX_AUTH_REFRESH_TOKEN`, `CODEX_AUTH_ACCOUNT_ID`, `CODEX_AUTH_ID_TOKEN`). The Codex row holds no other name (`agent.rs:207-212, 223`), and `Agent::ALL` has one entry. Every one of the five names starts with `CODEX_`, which `RESERVED_PREFIXES` already reserves, so the branch is fully redundant and is deleted. The reserved-name tests pass without it: `jackin-core` 148 of 148, which includes `ids::env_name_is_a_posix_name_and_reserved_names_are_detected` (`CODEX_HOME`, `CODEX_AUTH_ID_TOKEN`), `agent_table::codex_row_reserves_every_credential_env_name` (every credential name of the Codex row) and `config_edit::a_scoped_env_name_is_never_a_name_jackin_reserves`. The EN-1 break row above that removes `CODEX_` and "ignores the agent `reserved_env`" now reads as the `CODEX_` break alone. `AgentSpec::reserved_env` stays: the agent table test pins it and `API.md` documents it; nothing reads it for the check any more.

Base: `2b40cfb9c` (`origin/feature/openshell-native`) on `wt/P9.3-J1a`. Commits: `137436d96` (pin and `deny.toml`) and `1bae981d0` (the xtask steps and tests). ## J1a: decisions the design did not state, and deviations

1. **`version = "0.1.0"` on both lines.** The first `cargo deny check` printed `error[wildcard]: found 4 wildcard dependencies for crate 'jackin-tui'` (`bans.wildcards = "deny"` counts a git dependency with no `version`). The OpenShell lines carry `version = "0.0.0"` for the same reason. R1 does not look at `version`.
2. **First `cargo deny check` findings (all fixed in `deny.toml` or the lines above):** `error[duplicate]: found 2 duplicate entries for crate 'compact_str'` (0.9.1 from `ratatui-core`, 0.10.0 from `termpane`; the older one is skipped, as the design says), three `error[source-not-allowed]` for `detected 'git' source not explicitly allowed` (`junie-tui` and `jackin-console` at the baseline URL, `termpane`), and the wildcard error. After the fixes: `advisories ok, bans ok, licenses ok, sources ok`, exit 0. The new `Cargo.lock` also holds a second `darling` (0.20.11 and 0.24.1); deny did not report it.
3. **No other git dependency.** The lock has three git source URLs: `https://github.com/NVIDIA/OpenShell` (tag `v0.1.6`), the baseline (`junie-tui`, `jackin-console`) and `termpane`. `tuiscotti` is a dev-dependency of the baseline only; `grep -c tuiscotti Cargo.lock` prints 0.
4. **`cargo shear` needs a pointer.** `jackin-tui` does not use the two crates in code until J1b (`build_console`), and `cargo shear --deny-warnings` reports `unused dependency` and `unused workspace dependency` (`cargo deny` has `bans.workspace-dependencies.unused = "deny"`, so a workspace line with no member edge would fail there too; inferred, not run). `crates/jackin-tui/Cargo.toml` therefore has `[package.metadata.cargo-shear] ignored = ["jackin-console", "junie-tui"]`. J1b deletes it when the code names the crates.
5. **R6 lists three URLs**, not two, because of termpane (decision above). `ALLOWED_GIT` in `baseline.rs` is the single list; the test `allow_git_exact` accepts the three in any order and rejects a fourth, a missing baseline and a missing termpane URL.
6. **R4 `paths`.** Any non-empty `paths` array in `.cargo/config.toml` (or the root manifest) fails. The design says "a `paths` key that names" the URL or the crates, but a `paths` entry is a directory and the crate it holds can have any directory name, so a name test can be bypassed.
7. **Tests are one directory binary**, `crates/xtask/tests/gates/` (`baseline.rs`, `baseline_branch.rs`, `demo_gate.rs`, `layers.rs`, `screen.rs`, and the shared `workspace.rs`), not five top-level files. The workspace denies `dead_code` and `unreachable_pub`, and a helper module shared by several test binaries is dead in each binary that does not use all of it (the J0 note about `tests/common` and `cargo shear` points the same way). Nextest ids are `jackin-xtask::gates <module>::<test>`.
8. **`LAYERS` `depends` governs internal names only** (`is_internal`): `junie-tui` and `ratatui` are external, so listing them would be data with no effect and they are not listed. `layers_allow_the_console_crates` still proves that `jackin-tui` may depend on `junie-tui`, `jackin-console`, `ratatui` and `jackin-usage`. The bar on `termpane` and `vt100` applies to every member, not only `jackin-tui`: no jackin crate may name a terminal engine, the baseline owns it.
9. **`.git` suffix.** With one dependency line on `...baseline.git` and the other on `...baseline`, `cargo metadata` reports the `.git` spelling for both (inferred: cargo interns equal canonical URLs as one source id), so `dot_git_url_fails` expects two R1 violations.
10. **Tests added to the delta table:** `baseline::{testkit_path_dependency_fails, testkit_exemption_covers_demo_only, exemption_is_limited_to_testkit}` (N3), `baseline::renamed_forward_fails` (a renamed `jackin-console` must not forward `demo` unseen), `demo_gate::static_edge_fails` (design 3.7.5 item 1), `screen::stale_allowed_literal_fails` (N6) and the C1 rows of T20: `ratatui_allowed_imports_pass`, `ratatui_widgets_fails`, `ratatui_style_fails`, `ratatui_layout_fails`, `ratatui_group_import_fails`, `ratatui_prelude_fails`, `buffer_set_fails`. `repo_gates` gains `a_violation_in_baseline_or_screen_stops_before_the_later_tools` and two `only` cases; its `FULL` log grows by the three `cargo tree` calls of `demo-gate`, its `green()` fixture writes a conforming `deny.toml`, and `usage_edges_outside_the_rows_are_reported` no longer expects `jackin-tui` to be rejected for `jackin-usage` (that edge is allowed now; the test uses `jackin-usage-provider-core` instead).
11. **Not built.** `canary_in_binary_fails` and `xtask product-check` (the artifact half, J6). TK-1 (`ConsoleRig`, `Console::drain`, `click`, `status_is_neutral`, `reasons()`): `ConsoleRig` builds the console through `build_console` and `Console::drain` is part of J1b, so there is nothing to build it on yet; `jackin-testkit` does not depend on `jackin-console` yet, and its `LAYERS` row already allows it.
12. **The step list in `hk.pkl`, `mise.toml` (`check:repository-steps`) and `CONTRIBUTING.md` is not changed.** `baseline` and `screen` start no external tool and could join the `comments,layers,pins,containers,errors` list; that list feeds the generated CI check `repository-steps`, so it is left for the orchestrator.

## J1a: new tests, the named break of each, red and green

Method: each break is one edit to production code of the committed tree (`1bae981d0`), applied by a script (`harness/red.py` in the task scratchpad) that checks the old text occurs once, runs `mise exec -- mbx +1.99.1 nextest run -p jackin-xtask --no-fail-fast -E 'binary(gates) & test(=<name>)'` (`binary(repo_gates)` for the `ci_run` rows), restores the file from memory, and runs the same filter again. Every break compiles clean (the workspace denies unused code; breaks that left a variable or constant unused were extended with `&& false` or `|| true`), every red is an assertion (exit 100), every green exits 0, and `git status` after the run shows no file changed. "Red" is the first failing assertion, `left` before `right`; a long value is cut. Every one of the 63 tests of the `gates` binary has at least one break below.

Stub runs before the checks existed (the test files were written against a stub that reports nothing, then the check was written): baseline, `30 tests run: 4 passed, 26 failed` (the 4 passes are the positive tests `exact_pin_passes`, `testkit_exemption_passes`, `dev_edge_demo_passes`, `real_repo_passes`); screen, `16 tests run: 3 passed, 13 failed`; layers and baseline-branch against the unchanged `LAYERS` and a stub `baseline_branch`, `5 tests run: 1 passed, 4 failed` (`layers_reject_normal_testkit_edge` is green on the unchanged rows by design, it guards the rows and has break L03). The `demo_gate` tests were written after the code; their stub run (break DS, a check that reports nothing) is `11 tests run: 3 passed, 8 failed`, and the three positive tests are red under D01, D06 and D07.

## J1a: the named break of the design, on the real step

The design's break: the normal `jackin-console` edge of `jackin-tui` gets `features = ["demo"]`. Run with a built `target/debug/jackin-xtask` (the `xtask` alias passes `--locked`, and the edge changes the lock), then the manifest and `Cargo.lock` were restored byte for byte (`cmp`).

`jackin-xtask ci --only baseline`, exit 1:

```text
xtask: baseline
crates/jackin-tui/Cargo.toml: R3: jackin-console enables the feature demo, which the product build must not contain
xtask: baseline reported 1 violations
```

`jackin-xtask ci --only demo-gate`, exit 1:

```text
xtask: demo-gate
/Users/donbeave/Projects/jackin-project/wt/jackin-J1a/target/debug/deps/libjackin_console-b3435cc1df7b40ee.rlib holds a demo symbol: U core::ptr::drop_glue::<core::iter::adapters::map::map_fold<&jackin_console::domain::workspac...
/Users/donbeave/Projects/jackin-project/wt/jackin-J1a/target/debug/deps/libjackin_console-b3435cc1df7b40ee.rlib holds the demo canary "Claude Code v2.1.14"
/Users/donbeave/Projects/jackin-project/wt/jackin-J1a/target/debug/deps/libjackin_console-b3435cc1df7b40ee.rlib holds the demo canary "jackin-preview"
/Users/donbeave/Projects/jackin-project/wt/jackin-J1a/target/debug/deps/libjackin_console-b3435cc1df7b40ee.rlib holds the demo canary "payments-platform"
/Users/donbeave/Projects/jackin-project/wt/jackin-J1a/target/debug/deps/libjackin_console-b3435cc1df7b40ee.rlib holds the demo canary "tty004"
cargo tree -p jackin -e normal -i clap shows clap below jackin-console

cargo tree -p jackin-tui builds jackin-console with the feature demo
```

Green on the restored tree: `mise exec -- mbx +1.99.1 xtask ci --only baseline,screen,demo-gate` exits 0 (`xtask: baseline`, `xtask: screen`, `xtask: demo-gate`). `mise exec -- mbx +1.99.1 xtask baseline-branch` exits 0 and prints `74244972a1183db85111332ddab7eeabcbae81cb identical`.

## J1a: API.md and design notes for the orchestrator

- `API.md` section 8 (xtask): `CiStep` has 15 variants (`Baseline`, `Screen`, `DemoGate` after `Errors`); `xtask baseline-branch`; `BaselineCheck`, `ScreenCheck::{production, with_allowed}`, `DemoGateCheck<R>`, `CommandRunner`, `ToolOutput`, `SystemRunner`, `CompareSource`, `GhCompare`, `baseline_branch`, `pinned_revs`; `Dependency::{alias, source, features, uses_default_features}`, `Member::features`, `DependencySource`, `GitReference`.
- Design 3.3 R6 and PLAN P9.3 step 5: `allow-git` has the termpane URL until termpane is published; the dependency lines carry `version = "0.1.0"`.
- `decisions.tsv`: the termpane allow-git entry (operator authorization) and the `cargo-shear` ignore that J1b removes.

## J1: step J1b-part (pin, `build_console`, drain, bridge, read-only projection, tie, TK-1)

Task P9.3, jackin lane. Rest of step J1 of `/private/tmp/claude-501/plans/P9.3-design-v7.1.md` (section 7 J1 row, 4.2 to 4.5, 5.1, 6.1) with the test rows of `/private/tmp/claude-501/plans/P9.3-test-plan-v7.1-delta.md` (T36, T44, T50, and the v6 J1 rows T2, T7, T16, T21, T24, T27, T28). Base: `2fd532266` on `wt/P9.3-J1`. The baseline is pinned at `93949cf19e81d070582ecaf8895434c3cb5d2c05` (the coordinator moved the pin from `68f441d` during the run; `xtask baseline-branch` prints `93949cf19e81d070582ecaf8895434c3cb5d2c05 identical`). `jackin` with no arguments still prints help. `run_console` and `ConsoleHost` are not built (J6).

### J1: what changed

| Item | Change | Where |
|---|---|---|
| Pin | both D27 lines at `93949cf`; `Cargo.lock` changes only their two source lines and the new member edges | `Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock` |
| `build_console` | `async fn build_console(ConsoleDeps) -> Result<Console, ConsoleError>`: reads config, lists every gateway (up to `ConsoleLimits.connect_deadline`), projects the first `Snapshot`, builds `World` through `WorldParts` with the arbiter from that fleet, then calls the baseline `assemble` (`Entry::Manager`, `ClipboardMode::Host(HostClipboard)`, motion from `JACKIN_NO_MOTION` in the `EnvSnapshot`) | `crates/jackin-tui/src/console.rs` |
| `drain` and the bridge | `Console::drain` delivers queued external messages until `InFlight` is zero (replies are sent before the counter drops); `Bridge::spawn` runs an effect task on the tokio `Handle` and sends `Remote::Msg` through `ExternalSender` | `bridge.rs`, `console.rs` |
| Projection | pure `project(&ProjectInput, &mut IdTable) -> Snapshot`: workspaces, accounts, roles and trust rows, global env, instances from the fleet, `Surfaces::openshell` plus `.roles`, `.docker`, `.about`; every Fixed field comes from the baseline normalizers (`workspace`, `global`, `account`, `role`, `instance`, `env_edit_block`) | `project.rs`, `fleet.rs`, `observe.rs`, `ids.rs` |
| Backend | `JackinBackend: Backend`: `Effect::Refresh` is built (list, inspect and `work_state`, project, reply `Refreshed`); one refresh at a time; every other effect gets a `Completion::ready(0, Msg::..{ result: Err(Unbuilt text) })`; `validate`, `launch`, `spawn_pane` fail visibly (`Unbuilt`) | `backend.rs`, `unbuilt.rs` |
| Gateway health | `World.daemon_health` is `Stale` when any gateway failed to list; all gateways failed gives `Arbiter` `Err(IndexUnreadable)` (baseline text `Could not confirm running instances`); one failed gateway or unreadable labels give a status note | `observe.rs`, `console.rs` |
| TK-1 | `ConsoleRig` (`keys`, `click`, `click_text`, `paste`, `resize`, `tick`, `elapse`, `settle`, `screen`, `screen_at`, `status_line`, `status_is_neutral`, `reasons`, `skip_intro`), `FakeWall`, `World::seed_all`, `FixtureArgs`/`fixture_app` and the bin `console-fixture` | `crates/jackin-testkit/src/{console,console_fixture,world}.rs`, `src/bin/console-fixture.rs` |
| Testkit | `FakeGateway::find_sandboxes` honours `Fault::HoldAt { Trigger::Op(GatewayOp::Find) }` (TK-6 had no hold for the list) | `crates/jackin-testkit/src/gateway.rs` |
| Core | `ConfigStore::path()` | `crates/jackin-core/src/config/store.rs` |
| `screen` | `ALLOWED_LITERALS` = `JACKIN_NO_MOTION`, `git`, `path`; the stale-entry check runs only when `crates/jackin-tui/src` has Rust files | `crates/xtask/src/checks/screen.rs` |
| shear | the `ignored = ["jackin-console", "junie-tui"]` entry of `crates/jackin-tui/Cargo.toml` is gone | `crates/jackin-tui/Cargo.toml` |

### J1: decisions the design did not state, and deviations

1. **`build_console` is async** and `Console::drain` is async. The design shows sync signatures, but `block_on` inside a tokio test or the current-thread runtime panics. `ConsoleDeps` gains `wall: Arc<dyn WallSource>`, `home`, `version`, `theme`, `handle` and `limits`; it loses `ConsoleStart`. `config_path` comes from the new `ConfigStore::path()`.
2. **Wall time.** The core `Clock` has second resolution, and the baseline tick (`now_ms / 80`) would stutter, so production passes a `WallSource` (`SystemWall`, ms from `SystemTime`, offset from the core clock) and the rig passes `FakeWall` (core `FakeClock` plus a millisecond counter).
3. **`Refresh` is built at J1**, not J2. The manager issues it every 5 s (`manager.rs` `on_tick`), so failing it visibly would repeat an error forever, and it is read-only. Consequence for T44: the idle console runs one list per 5 s, not a doctor timer (J1b). `idle_console_is_quiet` asserts `inflight == 0`, no pane, no waiting reply and an empty status after every second of ticks. A refresh that finds every gateway failed replies `Err` (baseline text `Refresh failed: .. · showing last-good rows`) instead of an empty roster.
4. **Not built, reported:** `persist::edits` and T32 (J2: it writes), `run_console` and `ConsoleHost` (J6, no caller, a PTY is needed to test it), T14 (J3), the per-workspace Usage view of T7 (the key path was not found; `usage_page_lists_accounts_with_no_windows` drives the Usage overview), T21 cases 1, 9 to 17 (the nine cases that were driven are in item 6; the rest need the Accounts, wizard and file-browser key paths; case 20 is J5), the live part of T24 `new_workspace_defaults` (needs the wizard), T16 detail beyond roster, Settings, Accounts, Usage, Roles and the editor, and `inspect_changes`/`GitHub` (J2, J5). `canary_in_binary_fails` is J6.
5. **Tests were written after the code** (the API had to exist to compile them). Each test has a named break applied to production code by `harness/red.py` (task scratchpad), with the run before and after; see the table. Stub runs: none. The one exception is `fixtures_still_show_them`, which guards the other direction (no production break turns it red).
6. **T21 uses one test per control, key route only.** Each drives the baseline key path in the live console and checks: the status equals the `Fixed::openshell()` text (whitespace-normalised), it is neutral, the config bytes are unchanged, the status is gone after 40 ticks, an unrelated key (`down`, or `tab` on the Select) does not post it again, and `ctrl+s` writes nothing. The click route is not driven. Cases covered: keep_awake, git_pull, dirty exit, working directory, co-author, DCO, global mounts, trust toggle, agent modes (all six rows).
7. **`ALLOWED_LITERALS` grows by three** (`JACKIN_NO_MOTION`, `git`, `path`). `TrustRow.kind` is `&'static str` and the Settings screen compares it with `"git"` (`settings.rs:813` at the old pin), so jackin must supply those two exact strings. Test `production_allowed_literals_pass`. The other texts (`Note`, `Unbuilt`, the instance id prefix `jk-`, the About labels, the engine port) are in `Display` impls.
8. **Role manifests.** `Surfaces.roles` is projected. A `Path` role is read with `RoleCatalog::load_path` (cached per name and source); a `Git` role is not fetched at start and shows `the role manifest is read when the role is loaded`. Account providers `antigravity`, `hermes` and `muse` have no baseline `Provider`; their accounts are left out of the roster and a status note names each.
9. **Interim failing pieces.** `UnbuiltLaunch` emits `LaunchEvent::Failed` with a `FailedSetup` instance record (the baseline requires one), so a failed launch leaves one `failed setup` row until the next refresh. `UnbuiltPane` (`spawn_pane`) is a pane with an error note; `initial_panes` is empty. `HostClipboard::write` returns `false` (no native mechanism before J5), so a copy reports `Copy requested · this terminal may ignore OSC 52`. `validate` returns an outcome whose three rows say the check is not available; `probe_folder` returns `Unreadable`; `changes` returns no files.
10. **Instance status** is `Running` (Ready and supervisor `running`), `CleanExited`/`Crashed` (supervisor `exited n`, or `Completed`/`Error` with a code), `FailedSetup` (`Error` with no code), `RestoreAvailable` (everything else), and `PreservedDirty`/`PreservedUnpushed` from `work_state` for a ready sandbox whose supervisor is not running. `inspect` runs for every ready sandbox on every refresh; measure this at P9.5 (a lighter supervisor read in `jackin-session` would remove it).

### Baseline gaps (go back to the baseline, never into jackin)

- `OpError` has no variant for "this build cannot read 1Password"; `SignedInOnly` answers an empty account list and `Missing*` errors.
- `FailedLaunch.instance` is not optional: a launch that never created a sandbox still adds a `FailedSetup` row.
- `Backend::spawn_pane` cannot fail, `Backend::changes` and `Backend::probe_folder`/`validate` cannot say "pending" or "unavailable" (B5 pending results are not in the pin).
- `TrustRow.kind` should be an enum; `RoleSource::Git.branch` should be optional (an unpinned role shows `url @ `); `RoleEntry.namespace` has no jackin source (jackin uses the owner segment or the parent directory).
- The About rows (labels and values) are screen text that jackin supplies through `Display` impls; a `Vocabulary` field would keep them in the baseline. `DiscoveryError::label` (`instance index unreadable`) is still a constant, not `Vocabulary` (design 4.2).
- The error dialog `Next` row says `check disk space and permissions, then Save again` for every save error, including `not available in this build yet`.
- `Provider` lacks `Antigravity`, `Hermes` and `Muse`.

## J1b: doctor panel and `SessionConnector`

Task P9.3, jackin lane. Step J1b of `/private/tmp/claude-501/plans/P9.3-design-v7.1.md` (section 7 J1b row, 8, 9.3 `sessions.rs`) with design v6 section 11 and test row T15 (`/private/tmp/claude-501/plans/P9.3-test-plan-v6.md`), plus the T44 edit of `P9.3-test-plan-v7.1-delta.md`. Base: `3747c1a38` (J1 merged). The baseline pin is unchanged (`93949cf19e81d070582ecaf8895434c3cb5d2c05`). No baseline file was touched.

### J1b: what changed

| Item | Change | Where |
|---|---|---|
| `SessionConnector` | trait `connect(name, entry, cancel) -> Result<Session, GatewayError>`; production type `GatewaySessions` wraps a `GatewayConnector` and a closure that builds the `Session` over the connected gateway | `crates/jackin-tui/src/sessions.rs` |
| `SessionSet` | now holds its slots behind a mutex so `reconnect` can replace a `Failed` slot with a `Connected` one; `reconnect` retries only the failed gateways that the config still lists, concurrently | `sessions.rs` |
| `ConnectError::into_failure` | the discovery-to-`GatewayError` mapping moved from `jackin doctor` into `jackin-session`; the CLI calls it | `crates/jackin-session/src/connect.rs`, `crates/jackin/src/commands/doctor.rs` |
| Doctor mapping | pure `report(session report, gateway, at_secs)` to the baseline `DoctorReport`: label from `CheckId::label()`, status `Pass/Warn/Fail/Skip` to `Ok/Warn/Fail/Skipped`, `summary` and `found` from the detail (one line), `fix` from `CheckId::fix()` on `Warn` and `Fail` only, `gateway` is the default gateway when chosen | `crates/jackin-tui/src/doctor.rs` |
| Doctor state | `DoctorCell` holds the last report and a single-flight flag; every projected `Snapshot` carries `surfaces.doctor` (noun from the baseline `Vocabulary.health_noun`, state from the cell) so `Refreshed` never resets it | `doctor.rs`, `observe.rs` |
| Triggers | start (before the first snapshot, within `connect_deadline`, no reconnect); `Effect::RunDoctor` (the `D` page, key `r`) with reconnect; `Backend::due` every tick when the last report is older than 60 s, with reconnect | `console.rs`, `backend.rs` |
| Launch on a failed gateway | `JackinBackend::launch` reads the default gateway: when it is `Failed`, the failure view is the baseline `network_failure` with the gateway error as its detail (next step: the baseline `jackin doctor` text); nothing is created | `backend.rs`, `unbuilt.rs` |
| `ConsoleDeps` | gains `connect: Arc<dyn SessionConnector>`; `engine_socket` is now read; `ConsoleError::Pinned`; `Console` cancels its in-flight doctor on drop | `console.rs` |
| `Unbuilt::Doctor` | removed with the stub `RunDoctor` reply | `unbuilt.rs` |
| Testkit | `DoctorHost` (scripts `openshell --version`, `ssh -V`, `git --version`, `gateway list`, the gateway version), `EngineSocket` (a live unix socket), `connector_over`, `session_maker`, builder `host`, `connector`, `engine_socket`; the rig scripts a healthy host by default, so the J1 tests are unchanged | `crates/jackin-testkit/src/{doctor_host,console,lib}.rs` |

### J1b: decisions the design did not state, and deviations

1. **`SessionSet` is not borrowed.** The design shows `access(&self, name) -> GatewayAccess<'_>`. A retry has to change the set, and `GatewayError` is not `Clone`, so the slots sit behind a mutex, `get` returns an owned `Session`, `entries()` is gone, and the crate-private `Access` owns what `GatewayAccess` borrows. `GatewayState` stays the input form of `SessionSet::new`.
2. **No `SessionSet::connect` yet.** The first connection of every configured gateway (design 12) has no caller before J6, and the rig builds sets from `GatewayState` directly, so it would be untested code. J6 builds it from `SessionConnector`.
3. **The 60 s timer is `Backend::due`**, because the baseline has no doctor timer (the manager only issues `Refresh` every 5 s). `due` returns no message; it spawns the run through the bridge. A run in flight (from `due`, the key or the start) makes the others no-ops, and the one reply `DoctorFinished` resolves the page `Running` state.
4. **The start run does not reconnect.** J6 has just connected; a retry at start would also hide the failure the test shows. A start run that exceeds `connect_deadline` is dropped and the state stays `NeverRun` (the page shows `Checks have not run yet`); the next tick runs it.
5. **`expected` is `None`.** `jackin-session` has no expected text per check, and jackin may not add screen text. The detail pane shows the baseline dash.
6. **T15 wording.** "`elapse(59)` then `elapse(2)` without ticks" cannot show the chrome: the baseline `World::tick` reads the wall clock only on a tick. The test ticks once after each `elapse` and renders before `settle`, so it sees `stale` and then the rerun.
7. **`failed_gateway_retries`** injects the `Failed` slot through the builder and scripts the connector `[refused, ok]`: the first `r` still fails (calls 1, chrome failing), the second succeeds (calls 2, chrome healthy), and the next 5 s refresh lists the sandbox. The assertion `calls == 0` before the first `r` is the start rule of item 4.
8. **`launch_on_failed_gateway`** tests the failure rule of design 11 on the unbuilt launch; the real launch is J4 and keeps the rule.
9. **Extra test.** `held_doctor_waits_the_deadline` (not in the T15 list) guards the start deadline of item 4. `idle_console_is_quiet` (T44) now counts the doctor probes: two `openshell --version` and eight process calls in all.
10. **Tests were written after the code** (the API had to exist to compile them), as in J1. Each has a named break below. I had no subagent tool, so no independent reviewer saw the extra test plan or the red runs; the orchestrator must send them to `test-reviewer`.
11. **Not built:** the zero-gateway failing chrome (see gap 3), `SessionSet::connect` (item 2).

### Baseline gaps (go back to the baseline, never into jackin)

- `Backend::due` is documented for messages the backend raises; there is no `Effect`-issuing hook or doctor timer, so jackin spawns work from `due`.
- `Msg::DoctorFinished(DoctorReport)` cannot say "the checks could not run"; a cancelled run has no reply.
- `DoctorCheck.expected` has no jackin source (no per-check expected text in `jackin-session`).
- A config with no usable gateway gives `GatewayAccess::NotChosen`: the three gateway rows are `Skipped` and `health_view` is `Healthy`. T1 asks for a failing chrome; the baseline `failing()` counts only `Fail`.
- `World::apply` replaces `surfaces`, doctor state included, so every projection must carry it. A `Refreshed` projected just before a `DoctorFinished` and delivered after it shows the older report for up to 5 s.
- `health_view` ignores `daemon_health` once a report exists, so a failed listing on a connected gateway reaches the operator only as the status note.

Two first drafts did not compile (`dead_code` and a non-exhaustive match under the workspace `deny`) and were replaced by `LAUNCH-reason-dropped` and `LAUNCH-next-step-dropped`. "First" is the first line after the failing `panicked at` or `Error:`; a screen dump means the assertion message is the screen.

Task P9.3, jackin lane. This section records the fix round for findings 1, 2 and 4 of the J1a and J1 batch review (`/private/tmp/claude-501/plans/P9.3-J1-review.md`). ### J1 fix round: what changed

| Finding | Change | Where |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | R4 fails any `[source.*]` table that has `replace-with` or holds a string equal to the baseline or termpane URL (`.git` suffix and trailing slash ignored). The scan covers every `.cargo/config.toml` and `.cargo/config` in the repository, at the root and in member directories. | `crates/xtask/src/checks/baseline.rs`, `crates/xtask/src/repo.rs` |
| 2 | `read_fleet` bounds `list`, each `inspect` and each `work_state` with its own `connect_deadline`. A slow `list` marks only that gateway failed (`no answer within 1s`, named in the note). A slow `inspect` leaves the sandbox listed with `supervisor: None`; a slow `work_state` leaves it listed with `work: None`. | `crates/jackin-tui/src/fleet.rs`, `observe.rs` |
| 4 | Work reads of one gateway run at most `ConsoleLimits::work_reads` at a time. `WorkCache` keeps the `WorkState` per (gateway, sandbox) under the key (phase, `created_at`, supervisor reading) from `inspect`. | `crates/jackin-tui/src/fleet.rs`, `console.rs`, `observe.rs` |
| testkit | `FakeGateway::sandbox_detail` honours `Fault::HoldAt { Trigger::Op(GatewayOp::Detail) }`. `FakeGateway::calls(op)` counts the calls that reach the fault check. `ConsoleRig` passes `work_reads` 4. | `crates/jackin-testkit/src/gateway.rs`, `gateway/state.rs`, `console.rs` |

### J1 fix round: decisions the brief did not state, and deviations

1. **`.cargo/config` was not read at all.** The brief says the check already reads `config`; it did not. `FileKind::of` needs a file extension, so a `.cargo/config` never reached `Repo::files`. Cargo 1.99.1 still reads that file (probe: `cargo zz` on a scratch crate with `.cargo/config` printed ``.cargo/config` is deprecated in favor of `config.toml``, and the alias ran). `FileKind::of` now types `.cargo/config` as TOML, so the `comments` step also covers it.
2. **The other R4 cases follow the same file list.** `[patch]`, `[replace]` and `paths` are now also checked in member-directory configs and in `.cargo/config`, not only in the root `.cargo/config.toml`. The enabling condition was the same: only one path was examined.
3. **`[source.*]` rule.** `[source.vendored] directory = "vendor"` alone passes (it replaces nothing). `[source.crates-io] replace-with` fails, as the brief says "any".
4. **Deadline arithmetic.** There is no outer timeout per gateway. The worst case for one gateway is `list` + `inspect` + ceil(n / `work_reads`) work reads, each up to `connect_deadline`, and gateways run side by side. The held-list start still waits one deadline (`start::held_list_waits_the_deadline` passes).
5. **A late `inspect` shows `restore available`** (the `supervisor: None` path of review finding 9, the same as a failed `inspect`). That finding is not fixed here.
6. **Cache rules.** `WorkState::Unknown(_)` is never cached, so a transient failure is retried at the next refresh. A timed-out read is not cached. Entries for sandboxes missing from a successful read are dropped; entries of a failed gateway are kept. Staleness this accepts: a change on the host (a push, a new host commit) with an unchanged sandbox state is not seen until the sandbox state changes or the console restarts. The review proposed this key; the staleness is recorded here as the cost.
7. **Bound is a `Semaphore`, per gateway.** `buffered(n)` over `stream::iter(..).map(closure)` made `backend.rs:51` fail with `implementation of Send is not general enough` (a rustc higher-ranked closure limit; the annotated closure did not help). The bound is not covered by a test: the fake has no gauge of concurrent calls.
8. **`TimedOut` prints `{:?}` of the duration** (`1s`, `1.5s`), not `as_secs()`, so a deadline under 1 s no longer reads `0 s`.
9. **The new tests run in real time with a 1 s deadline.** `start_paused` cannot be used with `guard`: the 60 s guard timer is auto-advanced while the rig does blocking work, and the test fails with `the test hung for 60 s`. Each slow-gateway test takes about 1 s.
10. **Hold semantics.** A hold fires on the first arrival only, so `slow_inspect_keeps_the_sandbox_listed` holds one of two inspections and expects `[RestoreAvailable, Running]`.

### J1 fix round: new tests, red and green

Red runs are on the unfixed production code with the new tests and the testkit change in place (the xtask test against the old `baseline.rs`; the `fleet` binary against the old `fleet.rs` and `observe.rs`). Green runs are on the fixed tree.

| `jackin-tui::fleet slow_gateway_keeps_the_fast_listing` | `left: []`, `right: [Running]` | passed |
| `jackin-tui::fleet slow_inspect_keeps_the_sandbox_listed` | `left: []`, `right: [RestoreAvailable, Running]` | passed |
| `jackin-tui::fleet slow_work_read_keeps_the_sandbox_listed` | `left: []`, `right: [CleanExited, Running]` | passed |
| `jackin-tui::fleet work_state_is_read_once_per_state` | `an unchanged sandbox is read again`, `left: 18`, `right: 6` | passed |

### JBG: decisions the brief did not state, and deviations

1. **B5 and B7b changes forced more than the brief's list.** The pin removes `Backend::validate`, `probe_folder` and `changes` and adds four effects and `LaunchFeed::answer_env`. `ProbeFolder` answers `Ok(FolderProbe::Unreadable)` and `ReadChanges` answers `Ok(ChangeSet { files: [], unpushed })`, as before: the `Unbuilt` list has no subject for either, and jackin may not write one. `CheckSource` answers `Err(unbuilt ValidateAccount)`.
2. **The failed-setup row is gone.** `FailedLaunch.instance` is `None`, so `UnbuiltLaunch` no longer builds an `Instance` (`launch` ignores its `target`).
3. **Reruns are bounded.** The brief did not say when a doctor run is a `DoctorFailure`. I used the one deadline jackin has (`connect_deadline`) for the start run and the reruns. The production value is chosen at J6; a slow reconnect must fit in it.
4. **Notices on refresh.** A refresh with one failed gateway carries `Stale` in the snapshot but no status note (the `Notice` text is used at start and for the all-failed `Err`). Finding 3 asked for the health, and the chrome shows it.
5. **`idle_console_is_quiet` changed pace.** The loop was `tick(5)` and the rig advances about 1 s per `tick(5)`, so the loop spanned about 122 s, and the baseline timer fires at `>= 60 s` (the J1b rule was `> 60 s`). It gave three runs. The loop is now `tick(1)` (about 73 s), with the same expectation (two runs, eight calls).
6. **`RoleDocError::NotLoaded` still holds a sentence** (`the role manifest is read when the role is loaded`) in a `#[error]` attribute, which C2 allows by design; it reaches the Roles screen as `RoleRecord.manifest: Err(String)`. Not part of this step; a baseline gap (a typed reason).
7. **C2 `Display` rule is per file and by type name.** A `Display` impl and its `impl Error` must be in the same file, and two types of one name in a file count as one. `Debug` stays exempt (it is a diagnostic form, not a screen form).
8. **Tests were written after the code.** No independent reviewer saw the test plans or the red runs (no subagent tool); `test-reviewer` must review the new tests: `fleet::failed_gateway_shows_after_refresh`, `fleet::recovered_gateway_shows_healthy`, `doctor::no_usable_gateway_is_failing`, `doctor::held_rerun_says_it_could_not_run`, and `typed` (5 tests).
9. **Not proven:** `OpError::NotAvailable` for `LoadOp` (the 1Password picker is not driven by any test), the `next: None` of `SaveFailure` (the dialog without a `Next` row), `FailedLaunch.instance: None` (no break makes a row appear), the `work_reads` bound (J1 fix round item 7).
