## J2: persistence of every live edit

Task P9.3, jackin lane. Step J2 of `/private/tmp/claude-501/plans/P9.3-design-v7.1.md` (section 7 J2 row, 5.2) with design v6 section 8, and the test rows T2 (`cursor_survives_a_workspace_insert`), T3, T4 (config), T5, T6 (config), T17, T18, T19, T25 (round trip), T26, T28 (`o`), T31, T32, T33 and T38 (J), T40 (J) of the v6 and v7.1 plans. Base: `53e44ba26` (J1b merged). The baseline pin is unchanged (`93949cf19e81d070582ecaf8895434c3cb5d2c05`); no baseline file was touched.

### J2: what changed

| Item | Change | Where |
|---|---|---|
| Diff | pure `persist::workspace`, `persist::global`, `persist::accounts` take the saved `Config`, the payload and its `base` and return `Vec<ConfigEdit>` or `PersistError`; `Field` names what has no `ConfigEdit` (`Unrepresentable`); every record is destructured without `..` | `crates/jackin-tui/src/persist.rs` |
| Effects | `SaveWorkspace`, `SaveGlobal`, `SaveAccounts`, `DeleteWorkspace`, `Stop`, `Purge`, `LoadRole`, `CheckRole`, `SaveRole`, `BindRole`, `AddRole` (path), `RemoveRole`, `TrustRole` run on the bridge; each success reply carries the `Snapshot` of the saved config; each failure sends the reply with the error text and then `Remote::Reloaded` with the last saved config | `effects.rs`, `bridge.rs` (`Settled`), `backend.rs` |
| Accounts | edits go through `plan_account_edits` and `AccountPlan::apply` over `Gateways` built from the console sessions, so a Codex login account is synced to or forgotten from every gateway in the same sequence as the CLI | `effects.rs`, `sessions.rs` (`SessionSet::home`), `jackin-session/src/gateways.rs` (`Gateways::assemble`) |
| Stop, purge | `Session::power(Stop)` and `Session::purge(Guarded)`; the sandbox is found from the last observed fleet by instance id; the reply snapshot is a fresh `observe()` | `effects.rs` |
| GitHub origin | `Session::project_origin` per workspace path (60 s cache, all workspaces concurrently, bounded by the connect deadline) fills `Instance.github`, `Instance.default_branch` and `World.github` | `origin.rs`, `project.rs`, `observe.rs` |
| File source | `Backend::file_source` returns `HostFiles`, a `FileSource` over the host `FileSystem` | `files.rs` |
| Ids | `IdTable::rename` and `IdTable::name`; a renamed workspace keeps its id | `ids.rs` |
| Role manifest | a role whose directory has findings still shows its manifest when the manifest parses, so the check can run | `observe.rs` |
| Unbuilt | only `Restore`, `Prewarm`, `RefreshAccount`, `ValidateAccount`, `Launch`, panes and the Git role add still fail visibly | `unbuilt.rs` |
| Tests | `crates/jackin-tui/tests/console/{persisted,workspaces,accounts,roles,lifecycle,github,support}.rs` (one binary); the J1 `unbuilt_*` tests for the built effects are gone | `tests/console/` |

### J2: decisions the design did not state, and deviations

1. **The diff is three functions, not one `persist::edits`.** The three records need different resolved inputs (a canonical project path, a login read from `auth.json`), so `Resolved` carries them and the functions stay pure. The round-trip test (T32) calls all three.
2. **A stale base fails only when the target is gone** (5.2 rule 7): an untouched field keeps another handle's value (rules 1 to 3), the editor's change wins on a field both changed (rule 2). I read "a stale base is a visible failure" as the missing-target case, because T38 J requires the merge. Roles use the same rule.
3. **Rename order.** The edit list is `RemoveWorkspace(old)`, `AddWorkspace(new, copy of the saved entry)`, then the editor's edits, in one `ConfigStore::edit` (5.2 rule 5 lists the removal last). The core refuses two workspaces on one path, so the old key has to go first; the write is still atomic. The saved entry is read with `load` just before `edit`; a change by another handle between the two calls would be lost on a rename (`ConfigStore` has no closure form).
4. **Creation applies no Fixed check.** The wizard builds the workspace with `git_pull = true` (the baseline default), which the Fixed control refuses to show. Only an update compares Fixed fields with `base`.
5. **Account forms need J3.** The add and edit forms stop at `Backend::validate`, which is still unbuilt, so T5 add, source edit and the typed-input-kept cases are driven as `SaveAccounts` effects (config and gateway provider are the witnesses). Set-default, remove, workspace binding and role binding run through the screens.
6. **A new account is named from the display name**, an existing one by its id (the projection uses the name as the id). `purpose` and `endpoint` have no core field, so a typed value is `Unrepresentable`.
7. **Role add** reads a directory with `load_path` and registers it under the manifest name. A Git URL fails with `Adding a role from a Git URL is not available in this build yet` (the name is unknown before a checkout and `RoleCatalog` has no public probe). `TrustRole` answers with the current snapshot, because trust is Fixed (every source is trusted). `CheckRole` ignores the staged `doc` and checks the role directory, and its findings carry no `fix` text (jackin has none).
8. **Origin errors become "no origin".** A git failure in a workspace path shows no GitHub source; the session API reports the git error but the console has no place to show it.
9. **`HostFiles` blocks the key handler.** `FileSource::list` is synchronous, so the listing runs `FileSystem::list` under `futures::executor::block_on` inside the runtime handle. The baseline `FsSource` also reads on the key thread.
10. **Authored error text.** `PersistError`, `Refused` and `Field` carry short sentences in `#[error]` and `Display` (the `screen` step allows them). They are listed under the baseline gaps.
11. **Not built:** the T3 write fault through `MemFs` (the rig builds the console over `WorkingFs`, and a faulty config store needs a rig knob), the T4 launch half and T6 launch half (J4), the T17 Git role (item 7), the T18 forced purge (baseline gap 2), T33 `editor_save_status_asks_for_a_default_role` (baseline gap 1), the T26 launch-role cases (J4).
12. **Tests were written after the code**, as in J1, and each has a named break below. I had no subagent tool, so no independent reviewer saw the test plan or the red runs; the orchestrator must send them to `test-reviewer`. The harness (task scratchpad) applies one edit, runs the filter, restores the file and runs the same filter again. The first run of ten breaks did not compile (an unused variable under `deny`) and was redone with a compiling edit. `github::only_a_github_origin_counts` was vacuous in its first form (the fixture repositories were dropped before the console read them); the table row is from the fixed test.

### Baseline gaps (go back to the baseline, never into jackin)

1. `Fixed` has no `save_hint` at the pin, so the editor status `Saved {name} · set a default role in Roles` does not exist; T33 `editor_save_status_asks_for_a_default_role` cannot be written.
2. `Effect::Purge { instance }` has no force flag. The console can only ask for a guarded purge; unexported work is refused with the session text, and there is no way to confirm a forced purge.
3. `World::workspace_repo` finds the repository through the workspace's instances, so a saved workspace with no instance shows `No GitHub source` even when its origin is GitHub.
4. `FileSource` is synchronous (no pending result), see item 9.
5. The wizard creates the workspace with `git_pull = true` while the Fixed checkbox says always off; the editor shows it checked until the next reload.
6. The account form builds ids as `acct-{provider}-{name}`, while the snapshot ids are the account names, so `finish_saving` selects an id the snapshot does not hold (inferred from `accounts.rs`, not run: the add form needs J3).
7. `offer_for` preselects `ready.first()` for two accounts with no default, where `Config::select_account` refuses (`MissingBinding`); T6 asserts the core side only.
8. `RoleFinding.fix` and `key` have no jackin source; `Vocabulary` should hold the fix sentences.
9. The editor and the roles page refuse a taken workspace name and a role in use before sending the effect, so the core messages appear only when another handle changes the config first (the tests use that race).
10. The sentences of `Unrepresentable`, `Folder`, `NoProject`, `NoRoles` and the unbuilt effects are jackin text; a `Vocabulary` field for "this cannot be saved" would keep them in the baseline.

## J2 merge: JBG, OpenShell 0.1.7 and baseline BG2

Task P9.3, jackin lane. Branch `wt/P9.3-J2`. J2 (`765764643`, `75e722e55`) was merged with `origin/feature/openshell-native` twice: JBG (`d69a8cbb0`, console on baseline `516d522`) and OpenShell 0.1.7 (`38cb191`). Then the pin moved to baseline BG2 (`59d2a19b69aaade3481bdf966dd861b95d04e62b`, `xtask baseline-branch` prints `identical`) and J2 adopted its APIs. The J2 section above describes J2 at its own base `53e44ba26`; where it says "baseline gap", read the table below.

### J2 merge: how the conflicts were resolved

| File | Resolution |
| --- | --- |
| `backend.rs` | `Backend` lost `validate`, `probe_folder`, `changes` and `due`; `perform` routes `Restore`, `Prewarm`, `RefreshAccount`, `ValidateAccount`, `CheckSource`, `ProbeFolder`, `ReadChanges` and `LoadOp` to `unbuilt::fails` and every other effect to J2's `settle`; `file_source` (`HostFiles`) stays; `rerun_doctor` is JBG's. |
| `unbuilt.rs` | JBG's file, cut to the eight effects J2 does not build; the J2 per-effect functions and J1's `Unbuilt` copy are gone; `UnbuiltLaunch::new(world, failure)` is JBG's (`FailedLaunch.instance` is `None`). |
| `observe.rs` | `observe()` is `read_fleet(.., limits, &work)` (J1 fix round timeouts and `WorkCache`) followed by J2's `observation(config, fleet)`; `current()` reuses the stored `Fleet` (so `daemon_health` and notices survive a save); `manifest_only` stays; the J2 origin read is bounded by `limits.connect_deadline`; `Note`, `Notes`, `TimedOut` and `unmapped_accounts` stay deleted. |
| `project.rs` | JBG's typed values (`EngineEndpoint`, `TrustKind`, `daemon_health`, `instance_id`) plus J2's `ProjectInput.checks` and `origins`; J2's `instance_id(&SandboxName)` became `sandbox_id` (it returns the baseline `instance_id(suffix)`); `provider` is total, so `core_provider` gained `Antigravity`, `Hermes`, `Muse`; `FullName` (a `Display` impl outside an error type, which C2 rejects) became `GithubRepo::full_name` in `jackin-session`. |
| `tests/console/projection.rs` | J2's deletion of the six `unbuilt_*` tests of built effects wins; JBG's `words(..)` calls stay in the rest. |
| `P9.3.md` | both sections kept. |

Other adaptations: `Msg::WorkspaceSaved` and `GlobalSaved` carry `SaveFailure { error, next: None }`; `Field` became an error enum so its labels stay in `#[error]` attributes (C2), which BG2 then removed; `second_login` in `tests/console/accounts.rs` adds the account to the config before `sync_account`, because 0.1.7 holds the current account (`AccountError::Removed` otherwise); `tests/typed.rs` passes `checks` and `origins`.

### J2 merge: BG2 adoption

| Baseline BG2 item | jackin change |
| --- | --- |
| `Rejection`, `SaveField`, `Vocabulary::rejection` | `PersistError::{Unrepresentable, NoProject, Folder, NoRoles}`, `persist::Field`, `Refused::{NotInRoster, UnknownRole}` and `RoleDocError::NotLoaded` are gone. `PersistError::Rejected(Rejection)`, `Refused::Rejected(Rejection)` and `RoleDocError::Rejected(Rejection)` carry the value; their `#[error]` calls `Vocabulary::openshell().rejection(..)` (`persist::sentence`). |
| `Unbuilt::AddGitRole` | `Refused::Text(Vocabulary::openshell().unbuilt(Unbuilt::AddGitRole))`; jackin's own `AddGitRole` is gone. |
| `Workspace.github` | `project::workspace` takes the origin of the workspace and sets `github` with `repo_ref`; `persist::workspace` destructures `github: _`. |
| `UNSAVED_ID`, `AccountsSaved.added` | `persist::added(registry)` names the account with the empty id (its display name as `AccountName`); `save_accounts` replies `added: Some(name)` on success and `None` on failure. |
| `RoleFinding.fix: Option` | `None` (jackin has no fix text). |
| `Fixed.save_hint`, wizard `git_pull`, purge `next` | nothing from jackin. |

J2 error sentences still in jackin types: none. The pin to `59d2a19` removed the list that the JBG merge had left for BG2 (`Unrepresentable`, the folder errors, no project, no allowed role, not in roster, unknown role, role not loaded, the Git role add).

## J4: launch and cockpit from `Session::launch`

Task P9.3, jackin lane. Step J4 of `/private/tmp/claude-501/plans/P9.3-design-v7.1.md` (section 7 J4 row, 5.1) with design v6 (J4 row, BX6, 7.3, 9, 13) and the rows T8, T9, T4 (launch), T6 (launch), T16 (cockpit), T24 (cockpit branch), T25 (launch), T26 (picker and cockpit), T39 (`P9.3-test-plan-v7.1-delta.md` with the review amendments). Base: `f00631f32` on `wt/P9.3-J4`. The baseline pin is unchanged (`516d5228adf694e220182bceb999c4156bf7b22d`). No baseline file was touched.

### J4: what changed

| Item | Change | Where |
|---|---|---|
| Launch feed | `Backend::launch` starts a task (`Bridge::detach`, not counted by `InFlight`) that loads the config, resolves the workspace, takes the default gateway session, loads the role, plans with `plan_launch` and runs `Session::launch`. The baseline `LaunchFeed` reads a std channel on each `poll`; `answer_env` feeds an unbounded channel; `cancel` cancels a child of the console token | `crates/jackin-tui/src/launch.rs`, `backend.rs`, `bridge.rs` |
| Stage mapping | Exhaustive `LaunchStep` to the seven baseline stages: Image; Provider; Sandbox and Ready to Sandbox; Credentials; Upload; Supervisor and Hooks to Hooks; Start to Codex. A stage is Running at its first step, Done at its last step (Ready, Hooks) with `StageTimed` from `WallSource::epoch_millis`, and Skipped when every step of it was skipped. The per-stage activity is the baseline `StageSpec::activity_for`. `Image` lines become `BuildLine`; `SandboxKnown` becomes `ContainerReady`; the credentials step also sends `CredentialsResolved` | `launch.rs` |
| Env questions | `PlanError::EnvNeeded` becomes `NeedsInput` (prompt falls back to the variable name); the feed waits for `answer_env` or `cancel`, plans again with the answers | `launch.rs` |
| Refused | `RoleError::NotAllowed` and `PlanError::RoleNotAllowed` become `Refused`: `found` is the `PlanError::RoleNotAllowed` text, `state` is `LaunchWords.unit_missing`; `title` and `next_step` are empty (baseline gap 1). Nothing is created | `launch.rs` |
| Failed | A session failure becomes `StageChanged(stage, Failed)` and `Failed`: `summary` is the first line of `LaunchError::text()`, `detail` the other lines (the sanitized hook output), `next_step` the new `Display` of `LaunchNextStep`, and the sandbox name goes first as `ContainerReady`. Plan errors fail at a stage chosen by an exhaustive `PlanError` match. A gateway in `Failed` state keeps the J1b rule (baseline `network_failure` plus the gateway error). Credential failures and hook failures are `Failed`; `CredentialError` is never sent | `launch.rs` |
| Ready | `Ready` carries the instance the roster would show for the sandbox: `Session::inspect` and `work_state` through the J1 `project::instance`, so the D07 labels, account, workspace, workdir `/sandbox/project`, supervisor status and branch are the roster's. GitHub and `default_branch` come from `Session::project_origin` (best effort). `World.daemons` stays empty until `Go::Attach` | `launch.rs`, `observe.rs`, `project.rs` |
| Failure and cancel | `FailedLaunch.instance` and the `cancel` record are `None`; the task then asks for a refresh, so the roster lists the sandbox that remains | `launch.rs` |
| Current directory | A directory with no saved workspace is planned against an in-memory workspace named after the directory (`Config::apply(AddWorkspace)` on a clone); nothing is saved | `launch.rs` |
| `LaunchNextStep` | `Display` moved from `crates/jackin/src/report.rs` into `jackin-session`; the CLI text is unchanged | `jackin-session/src/report.rs`, `jackin/src/report.rs` |
| Removed | `UnbuiltLaunch`, `launch_failure` (the launch is built); `projection::unbuilt_launch_fails_visibly` | `unbuilt.rs`, `tests/console/projection.rs` |
| Dependencies | `jackin-image` for `ImageEvent` (the `LAYERS` row already allows it) | `crates/jackin-tui/Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock` |
| Testkit | `ConsoleRigBuilder::cwd` | `crates/jackin-testkit/src/console.rs` |

### J4: decisions the design did not state, and deviations

1. **No sentence is written in jackin, with one exception.** Every shown text is a typed value: the baseline `LaunchWords`, `StageSpec`, `network_failure`, the `Display` of session and core errors, `LaunchNextStep`. The exception is `Unlaunchable::WorkspaceGone` (`#[error("the workspace is no longer in the config")]`), which the `screen` check allows in an `#[error]` attribute; no existing typed error fits a workspace that another handle removed. The `screen` step passes with `ALLOWED_LITERALS` unchanged.
2. **T23 is deleted in v7.1** (replaced by T44, which has no J4 addition), so there is no replay list to extend.
3. **J5 rows.** T8 "the capsule shows text the fake main process printed" and the pane suffix need panes; `spawn_pane` still answers the unbuilt reply and `initial_panes` is empty. The Ready tests assert the route is `Capsule` and that `Go::Attach` built the daemon entry for the instance.
4. **No console retry.** T9 "the retry ends running" has no path: every console launch gets a new launch ID, and there is no key that relaunches an ID. The CLI retry is covered by the session tests. T39 `retry_credentials`: the jackin feed ignores it, because a credential failure is a `Failed`, never a `CredentialError`; the assertion belongs to the baseline test feed.
5. **Launch tasks are not counted by `InFlight`** (design 6.10). A test waits with `until` (tick, `settle`, check). The refresh after a failure or a cancel is counted.
6. **`cancel` is the only cancel path.** There is no `Drop`. `Console` shutdown cancels the root token, and the launch token is its child.
7. **GitHub mapping is private to `launch.rs`** (`repo_ref`, owner and name from `GithubRepo`). J2 owns the origin work; its mapping should replace this one at the merge.
8. **Image build lines use `LineDelivery::BestEffort`**, so a slow poll drops lines, not the launch.
9. **After Esc or an acknowledged failure with no running instance the baseline goes to `Outro`**, not the manager (`Go::LaunchFailedAck`). Tests that need the manager start from a console with one running sandbox (`rig.launch(1)`).
10. **Tests were written after the code** (the API had to exist to compile them). I had no subagent tool, so no independent reviewer saw the test plan or the red runs; `test-reviewer` is needed. The harness is `red.py` in the task scratchpad: one text edit to production code per break, the filter run, the file restored and its hash compared.
11. **Changed J1b test.** `doctor::launch_on_failed_gateway` waits for the failure (the launch is a task now). Its two breaks are re-run below.
12. **Not proven:** the entropy failure feed (`unlaunched`), the `NotChosen` gateway branch, the `Ready` inspect failure branch, a workspace that parses badly. `esc_at_the_picker_creates_nothing`, `without_a_default_role_every_row_is_disabled` and `the_current_directory_with_several_roles_asks_for_a_workspace` test baseline logic that no jackin edit reaches, so they have no named break.

### Baseline gaps (go back to the baseline, never into jackin)

1. `Refusal` has no typed words. `title` and `next_step` exist only in the demo-gated `sim/launch.rs::opening`. The refusal view has an empty title and an empty `Next step` row.
2. `LaunchFailure.next_step` is a `String`, not an `Option`: a failure with no remaining sandbox shows an empty `Next step` row.
3. The failure dialog clips `summary` and `Next step` to one line with `…` although the property is `.wrap()` (`Next step  sandbox jackin-platfor-szho remains; retry with: j…`).
4. `World::offer_for(None)` (a directory with no saved workspace) offers an account only when a provider default exists. With one account and no default, D26 says "the only account", but the picker shows `No matches` and the launch cannot start. `the_current_directory_launches_with_the_only_role` sets a default.
5. `Go::LaunchFailedAck` and a cancel with no running instance end in `Outro`, not the manager.
6. `LaunchTarget.agent` other than Codex launches Codex: there are no typed words for a refusal, and the picker disables those rows.
7. `LaunchFeed::cancel` is synchronous and cannot wait for the sandbox, so the record of a cancelled launch reaches the roster only with the refresh.
8. `LaunchEvent::Warning(DockerCapability)` has no baseline counterpart; the cockpit draws the Docker banner from `Surfaces.docker`, and the four risks from the `i` dialog.

### J4: new tests, the named break of each, red and green

Method: `red.py`, one edit per break; the filter is the test or tests named; the file is restored and its sha256 checked; the full suite is run green at the end. A first draft of five breaks did not compile (an unused variable or a needless `mut`) and was redone as a compiling edit. `ENV-launch-before-answer` alone stayed green (cancel still stops the launch), so the Esc test is broken with `ENV-launch-before-answer+CANCEL-not-wired` (the launch starts before the answers and the cancel does nothing); its test then waits for a second sandbox. "First" is the first line after the failing `panicked at` or `Error:`; a screen dump means the assertion message is the screen. Six reds are `the test hung for 60 s`: the test waits for a screen that never appears.

## J3: pending validation, the 1Password flow up to usage, and the usage mapping

Task P9.3, jackin lane. Step J3 of `/private/tmp/claude-501/plans/P9.3-design-v7.1.md` (section 7 J3 row: pending validation, 1Password, usage mapping; needs B5 and J2) with design v6 sections 7.2, 7.3 and 10, and the test rows T14, T30 and T7 of `P9.3-test-plan-v6.md` with the amendments of `P9.3-test-plan-v7.1-delta.md` and `-review.md`. Branch `wt/P9.3-J3`, base `8ecd57b69` (the console account edits planned under the account lock, the fix that makes the J2 merge compile). The baseline pin is unchanged (`59d2a19b69aaade3481bdf966dd861b95d04e62b`); no baseline file was touched.

### J3: what changed

| Item | Change | Where |
|---|---|---|
| `op` behind one interface | `OnePasswordCli` runs `op account list`, `op vault list`, `op item list` and `op item get`, each with `--format json` and `--account` only when an account is named, through `ProcessRunner::run_secret`. The JSON is parsed in one function and dropped (the `Document` wiper of `credentials.rs`); a concealed field keeps its id, label, kind, section and a four-character tail, never its value. A failed `op` is classified by markers in stderr (`OpFault`); the stderr text is never kept or shown. | `crates/jackin-session/src/op.rs` |
| Source checks | `SourceDesk::check(Source, cancel)`: `Env` (valid name, set, not blank), `File` (regular file, following links), `Folder { login }` (directory, and for a Codex account a readable ChatGPT login), `OnePassword { account, reference }` (shape `op://vault/item[/section]/field` first, then the item through `op`, the field must exist, be concealed and not be empty). `SourceDesk::probe_folder` names what a folder holds (`FolderFacts`). | `crates/jackin-session/src/source.rs` |
| Effects | `CheckSource`, `ProbeFolder`, `ReadChanges`, `LoadOp`, `RefreshAccount` and `ValidateAccount` run on the bridge; their replies carry the request ticket (or the account id). `unbuilt::fails` keeps only `Restore` and `Prewarm`. | `backend.rs`, `checks.rs`, `onepassword.rs`, `usage.rs`, `changes.rs`, `effects.rs` |
| 1Password picker | `OpSnapshot` (public, derived `Debug`) is the `OnePassword` object of the world and the cache of what `LoadOp` listed (names, ids, field kinds, tails). `reference` and `describe` answer from it, so a non-concealed field is `OpError::WrongFieldShape` and an empty one `OpError::EmptyMaterial`, both with the baseline wording. A locked `op` sets `OpSession::Locked`. `SignedInOnly` is deleted. | `onepassword.rs`, `console.rs`, `unbuilt.rs` |
| Form check | `ValidationOutcome` for `CheckSource`: `MaterialDiscovered` when the source is found, issue code, lifecycle and recoverability from the fault; op faults take the baseline `OpError::message()`. A plain key is refused with `Rejection::CannotSave(ApiKey)`. | `checks.rs` |
| Usage | `RefreshAccount` and `ValidateAccount` call `HostUsage::report` for the one account with `Refresh::Force`, one report at a time. `LimitWindow` becomes `QuotaWindow` (id from `WindowKind::id`, `used_pct` from `Percent::rounded`, category FiveHour = Session, Weekly and Monthly = LongRange, others Other, `Remaining::Amount` to `spend_label`). `Unavailable(reason)` becomes one sentinel row with the reason text and an `IssueCode` (ExpiredLogin to Unauthorized, NoCredentials to CredentialFileMissing, RateLimited, Network and Provider to ProviderUnavailable with `Failed`, Unsupported to QuotaUnsupported). `Stale` keeps the windows. | `usage.rs`, `jackin-usage/src/model.rs` |
| Changes | `read_changes(session, sandbox, unpushed, cancel)` maps `Session::changes` to the baseline `ChangeSet`. | `changes.rs` |
| Wiring | `ConsoleDeps` gains `usage: Arc<HostUsage>`; `Inputs` holds the `SourceDesk`, the `OnePasswordCli`, the `OpSnapshot` and a usage gate. The testkit builds a `HostUsage` over `world.usage` and the world runner for every `ConsoleRig`. | `console.rs`, `observe.rs`, `jackin-testkit/src/console.rs` |
| Drain | `Console::drain` stopped as soon as the in-flight count was zero at the start of a pass, so an effect started by a delivered reply (`CheckSource` then `ProbeFolder` then `SaveAccounts`) was not awaited. It now loops until the count is zero after a delivery. | `console.rs` |
| Smaller | `jackin-usage` `HostUsage::report` builds its futures first and buffers them (the closure form was not `Send` for a spawned task); `WindowKind::id`; `Percent::rounded`; `Config::require_account` is public; `crates/jackin-session/src/op.rs` joins `EXPOSE_ALLOWLIST`. | `host.rs`, `model.rs`, `edit.rs`, `layers.rs` |

### J3: decisions the design did not state, and deviations

1. **`op` failures are classified from stderr, which is never kept.** Design 10 says "Locked and AuthorizationRequired come from the exit status class". The real `op` exits with 1 for every failure, so the exit status cannot tell them apart. `classify` lower-cases stderr inside a `Zeroizing` string, matches fixed markers (signed in, authorization, permission, "isn't a vault", "isn't an item", "isn't an account") and drops it. No variant of `OpFault` holds stderr text, and the tests give every failing reply a canary in stderr. The markers are my reading of `op` 2.x messages, not a recorded transcript; this is the part to check against a real `op` (the e2e lane).
2. **A four-character tail is kept for a concealed field**, as design 10 says, so the form can show `••••••••…d2e8`. It sits in `OpSnapshot` and prints in its derived `Debug`. The full value is parsed from the `op item get` JSON, turned into the tail and wiped with the document. If the tail is judged too much, `describe` returns `masked("")` and nothing else changes.
3. **The picker needs a shared cache.** `OpFlow` chooses a field through the synchronous `OnePassword::reference` and `describe` on `World.op`, with no reply message. So `OpSnapshot` is an `Arc<Mutex<..>>` that the world and the backend both hold. `OnePassword::accounts()` returns a borrowed slice that a shared cache cannot lend, so it is empty and the picker always starts at the account step.
4. **Vault access and counts.** `op vault list` has no access flag and no item count. The vault row asks `op item list` for each vault: a forbidden answer is `VaultAccess::Denied`, any other answer is `ReadWrite` (read-only cannot be seen) and the item count is real. That is one `op` call per vault.
5. **Item rows have no field count** (`op item list` does not give one). `OpItemRow.fields` is 0, so the picker line reads `{category} · 0 fields`. See the baseline gaps.
6. **Validation is two things.** `CheckSource` (the form button and the save) checks that the source exists and is readable; it never calls a provider, so its level is at most `MaterialDiscovered`. `ValidateAccount` (the Validate action on a saved account) asks `HostUsage` and reaches `QuotaReadable` when windows come back.
7. **`ProbeFolder` and the folder check are in `jackin-session` (`SourceDesk`), not `Session`.** `Session` is bound to one gateway; the folder and `op` are host facts. The brief said "through Session"; I read it as "through jackin-session".
8. **A non-Codex local folder passes the check when it is a readable directory**, because `persist::credential` stores it as `CredentialSource::File { path: <the directory> }`. `jackin-usage` reads a `File` credential as a text file, so such an account can never read usage. This is a J2 problem the check cannot fix (`Source::File` is built and tested for `--credential file:` accounts, and the console does not use it).
9. **Refreshes run one at a time** behind a mutex, and `stagger_ms` is ignored: `HostUsage::report` loads, changes and saves one cache file, so two reports at once could lose an entry.
10. **`RefreshAccount` forces the provider call** (`Refresh::Force`); the user pressed refresh. `ValidateAccount` forces it too.
11. **Skipped check rows carry an empty string.** `CheckRow::Skipped(String)` has no typed form (baseline gap 1).
12. **`ReadChanges` and `ProbeFolder` are public functions (`read_changes`, `folder_probe`)**, because their reply messages cannot be observed through the rig (the capsule that sends `ReadChanges` is J5) and the form only uses the probe result to pick a `DetectedKind` that is not stored. The tests call the functions with a real `Session` and a real `SourceDesk`.
13. **Tests were written after the code**, as in J1 and J2, and each has a named break below. I had no subagent tool, so no independent reviewer approved the plan or saw the red runs; `test-reviewer` must review them. The harness (task scratchpad) applies one edit, runs the filter, restores the file and runs the same filter again.
14. **T23 "extended with the B5 sequences" has no row.** v7.1 replaces T23 by T44 (`idle_console_is_quiet`, J1), and no B5 key sequence exists in jackin's tests. Nothing was built for it.
15. **T30 provider list is not built** (baseline gap 2). The Edit gating, the live labels, the preselection and the `op_note` are tested.
16. **`jackin-session/src/op.rs` is added to `EXPOSE_ALLOWLIST`.** `SecretBytes::expose` is called once, to parse the `op` JSON.

### Baseline gaps (go back to the baseline, never into jackin)

1. `RecoverableIssue.message`, `CheckRow::{Ok, Failed, Skipped}` and `Refresh.outcome` are free strings, and `Vocabulary` has no words for them. jackin fills them from text that already exists in a typed place (`OpError::message()`, the `UnavailableReason` display of `jackin-usage`, `#[error]` text of `SourceFault`, `Freshness::label()`, `DetectedKind::label()`) and from an empty string for a skipped row. Typed rows and issue words in `Vocabulary` would remove every authored sentence from the jackin crates.
2. `AccountForm.providers` is fixed to Claude Code, Codex, Grok Build and OpenCode. Cursor, Gemini, Openrouter and the other providers the projection emits cannot be added from the console, so T30's provider-list case cannot pass and the usage-only token providers have no form.
3. `OpItemRow.fields` and `OpVaultRow.access` have no source in `op`: the picker prints `0 fields` for every item and cannot show `read-only`. They should be `Option`, or the lines should leave them out.
4. `OnePassword::accounts()` returns a slice borrowed from the object, which a cache shared with the backend cannot lend. A listing the flow asked for through `LoadOp` should also reach `reference` and `describe` through the reply, not through a second object.
5. `ChangeSet` has no `omitted` count and `DiffFile` no unread flag, so `Session::changes` loses both. `Effect::ReadChanges` carries `touched` and `uncommitted`, which nothing needs.
6. `Effect::RefreshAccount { stagger_ms }` asks the backend to delay; the clock and the cache decide that in jackin.
7. Nothing in the rig observes a reply message, so `ChangesRead` and `FolderProbed` are tested through the public functions behind them.

## J4 on BG3: launch and cockpit on baseline 6870a19

Task P9.3, jackin lane. Base: `d50f2ef19` on `wt/P9.3-J4`. Both D27 pin lines in `Cargo.toml` (`jackin-console`, `junie-tui`) move from `516d5228adf694e220182bceb999c4156bf7b22d` to `6870a19082aa00e7719120c067d34ef83ab80d6a`; `xtask baseline-branch` prints `6870a19082aa00e7719120c067d34ef83ab80d6a identical`. The baseline `docs/P9.3-red-green.md` sections BG2, B8b and BG3 were read. The baseline repository was only read (`git show`, `git grep`).

### What changed

| Gap (J4 "Baseline gaps") | Before | Now | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 Refusal words | `Refused(Box<Refusal>)` with the planner text and an empty title and next step | `Refused(RefusalReason::RoleNotAllowed { workspace, role })`; jackin writes no refusal text | `launch.rs` |
| 2 Next step | `LaunchFailure.next_step` was the `Display` of `LaunchNextStep`, empty when none | `NextStep`: `Retry { sandbox, launch }` for a remaining sandbox, `FixCredential` for a credential failure (`LaunchError::Credential`, or `ProviderUnavailable` over `AccountError::Credential`), `RunDoctor` for a failed gateway (the `network_failure` of `LaunchContent::openshell()`), `None` otherwise | `launch.rs` |
| 3 Clipping | none in jackin | none | |
| 4 One account, no default | tests set a default | no jackin code; `the_current_directory_launches_with_the_only_role` keeps its default (not changed) | |
| 5 Landing | tests started from a console with one running sandbox | `LaunchContent::openshell()` (already what `Backend::launch_content` returns) says `Manager`; the `running_console` and `fresh` test helpers are deleted | `tests/launch/*` |
| 6 Other agent | launched Codex | `Refused(AgentNotLaunchable { agent, launchable: Codex })` before any stage, before the config is read | `launch.rs` |
| 7 Cancel record | `cancel` returned `None` and the task asked for a refresh | `cancel` returns `None`; when the session returns `Cancelled` with a sandbox, the task builds the record from `Session::inspect` and `work_state` (the roster's own `instance_of`) and sends `Msg::LaunchCancelled { instance }` through the new `Bridge::send`. No sandbox: nothing is sent. The record cannot be built: the old refresh | `launch.rs`, `bridge.rs` |
| 8 Docker | banner from `Surfaces.docker` only | `Warning(DockerCapability { risks })` for `LaunchWarning::DockerCapability`, with the D24 risks from `DockerRisk::ALL` (`project::docker_risks`, shared with `Surfaces.docker`) | `launch.rs`, `project.rs` |

Deleted workarounds: `LaunchNextStep: Display` in `jackin-session` and its `failure_text` assertion (the CLI text is back in `crates/jackin/src/report.rs`, byte for byte as before J4); the empty `title` and `next_step` of the refusal and its `state` from `unit_missing`; the `String::new()` next step; the refresh after a cancel; the `running_console` and `fresh` helpers.

Kept: `Unlaunchable::WorkspaceGone` (`the workspace is no longer in the config`). The baseline has no typed home for it: `Rejection` has `NoProject` (`a workspace needs a project folder`), `NotInRoster` (an instance) and `NoRoleLeft`, none of which says that a workspace left the config. The `screen` check still allows it.

Compile-only changes for BG2 and B8b (no behavior): `Msg::AccountsSaved.added: None` in the unbuilt reply, `AppOptions.panes: PanePolicy::default()`, `PaneBody::note(&self, _words: &PaneWords)`. J2's persistence (merged on `feature/openshell-native` at 59d2a19) is not rebuilt here; the merge will meet it in `unbuilt.rs` and `project.rs`. `Workspace.github` stays `None` from `Workspace::new` until that merge.

### Decisions

1. A credential failure is `FixCredential` even when a sandbox remains. The credential must be fixed before a retry can work, and the failure dialog still names the sandbox. A launch that fails for another reason with a sandbox left is `Retry`.
2. The agent check runs first in the launch task, so a non-Codex target is refused whatever its role.
3. A failed record (`Session::inspect` fails after a cancel) falls back to the old refresh. Not proven.
4. `Ready` that fails to inspect leaves the sandbox but sends `NextStep::None` (as before, with an empty row then). Not changed; see the suggestions in the report.

### Tests and breaks

Changed: `picker::a_role_removed_under_the_picker_is_refused` (title, found, state and next step from `LaunchWords::refusal`, then Esc goes to the manager), `failure::provisioning_failure_keeps_the_sandbox` (the retry sentence from `LaunchWords::next_step`, no earlier running sandbox), `failure::cancel_during_upload_keeps_the_sandbox` (the record of the cancelled sandbox is on the roster with no tick, `until_untouched`), `failure::cancel_before_the_sandbox_leaves_none` (the manager, not the outro), `failure::esc_in_the_env_form_creates_nothing` and `failure::a_failing_hook_shows_its_clean_output` (no earlier sandbox), `docker::docker_without_an_engine_fails_and_creates_nothing` (no `Next step` row), `doctor::launch_on_failed_gateway` (the `RunDoctor` sentence), `fixture_mode::console_fixture_args` (the baseline scenario `capsule-terminal` opens the capsule; it failed on the new pin with `assertion failed: app.route() != Route::Capsule || scenario == Scenario::CapsuleMulti` before the exemption).

New: `picker::another_agent_is_refused_before_any_stage`, `failure::a_launch_that_leaves_nothing_returns_to_the_manager`, `failure::a_missing_login_fails_with_the_credential_next_step`, `docker::a_docker_launch_warns_though_the_roster_did_not_know` (the config turns Docker on after the roster loaded, so only the event can raise the banner).

First attempt at the cancel test was green with the record dropped: the manager refreshes itself every 5 s of world time and the tick loop crossed it. The final test waits without ticks, so only the message can put the record on the roster. Method: `breaks.py` (scratchpad `j4bg3/`), one text edit per break, the file restored and its sha256 compared (all `restored: true`). Two first drafts (`CANCEL-record-dropped`, `DOCKER-warning-dropped`) did not compile (unused imports) and were redone as compiling edits.

| Break | Result | Failing tests | First |
|---|---|---|---|
| `REFUSE-fields-swapped` (workspace and role swapped, both sites) | red | a_role_removed_under_the_picker_is_refused | `Error: "the test hung for 60 s"` (the screen never shows `does not allow role sentinel`) |
| `LANDING-outro` (`when_empty: Outro`) | red, 5 failed | a_launch_that_leaves_nothing_returns_to_the_manager, cancel_before_the_sandbox_leaves_none, esc_in_the_env_form_creates_nothing, provisioning_failure_keeps_the_sandbox, a_role_removed_under_the_picker_is_refused | `assertion `left == right` failed` (Outro against Manager) |
| `AGENT-always-launched` (no agent check) | red | another_agent_is_refused_before_any_stage | `[NeedsInput([EnvQuestion { name: "GREETING", .. }])]` |
| `NEXT-retry-dropped` | red | provisioning_failure_keeps_the_sandbox | expected `sandbox jackin-platfor-mnoi remains; retry with: jackin load --launch-id rbgnzvxtedp7aks4pez4zbwu2n` missing from the dialog |
| `NEXT-retry-swapped` (sandbox and launch swapped) | red | provisioning_failure_keeps_the_sandbox | expected `sandbox jackin-platfor-5tpq remains; retry with: jackin load --launch-id rextznpd6tg4samfyteoek7faa` missing |
| `NEXT-row-always` (`RunDoctor` for a plan failure) | red | docker_without_an_engine_fails_and_creates_nothing | `assertion failed: !shown.contains("Next step")` (screen dump) |
| `NEXT-credential-dropped` | red | a_missing_login_fails_with_the_credential_next_step | expected `Fix the account's credential in Accounts, then launch again.` missing |

Not proven: the fallback refresh when the cancel record cannot be built, the `Ready` inspect failure, a workspace that left the config, and the `Retry` after a credential failure that leaves a sandbox (decision 1).

## J4 merge: J2 and J3 into P9.3 J4

Task P9.3, jackin lane. Merge of `origin/feature/openshell-native` (`c40f9990f`, J2 persistence, J3 sources, 1Password and usage) into `wt/P9.3-J4` (`7a1380f5c`, J4 on baseline `6870a19`). Merge base `f00631f32`. The pin stays `6870a19082aa00e7719120c067d34ef83ab80d6a` (it contains `59d2a19`); both D27 lines in `Cargo.toml` and the two baseline sources in `Cargo.lock` keep it, and the OpenShell 0.1.7 lines come from origin. The baseline sections B8b, the batch fix round and BG3 change only panes (`LinkSource::restart`, `PaneWords`, `PasteOutcome::Full`) and the launch API J4 already adopted, so no J2 or J3 code needed an adaptation.

| File | Resolution |
|---|---|
| `Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock` | J4's pin on both D27 lines and both lock sources; origin's OpenShell 0.1.7. |
| `unbuilt.rs` | J4's file with `fails` cut to `Restore` and `Prewarm` (the effects J5 and a later step still build), the J3-built arms gone, `UnbuiltLaunch` and its helpers gone, and `SignedInOnly` gone (J3's `console.rs` passes the real `op` snapshot). |

| `project.rs` | Origin's `Origins`, `repo_ref(&GithubOrigin)` and `instance(.., origins, ..)`; `instance` is `pub(crate)` for the launch record. |
| `launch.rs` | J4's private `repo_ref(&GithubRepo)` is deleted. `ready` reads `Session::project_origin`, keys it by the workspace name, and `instance` fills `github` and `default_branch` as J2 does for the roster. |

| `effects.rs` | Unchanged: `AccountsSaved.added` stays `Some(name)` for an unsaved account; J4's `added: None` existed only in the `unbuilt.rs` arm that is gone. |

## J5: Capsule panes on OpenShell sessions

Task P9.3, jackin lane, step J5. Branch `wt/P9.3-J5`. The brief named the base `5af16cf6c`; the branch starts at `bfe2f93ea` (decision 1). Both D27 lines are at `9ac0e55f6c15c9ae9adb026d5fa8652e0a4c0748` (the baseline B8b step and its batch fix round). Commits: `dff9486cb` (J3's compile fix, picked), `5386ebd8a` (P-12), `e9410eeec` (clipboard writer), `7b4a42313` (testkit), `d4c7cd099` (the pane hub, the pin and the console wiring), `a2eb21ad5` and `cfd6be690` (test hardening and two new rows). `xtask baseline-branch` prints `9ac0e55f6c15c9ae9adb026d5fa8652e0a4c0748 ahead`, not `identical`: the baseline `feature/openshell-native` has moved five commits past `9ac0e55` since the brief (`gh api` compare: `behind_by=5`, `ahead_by=0`), and the step accepts `ahead`.

### J5: what changed

| Item | Change | Where |
|---|---|---|
| Pin | both D27 lines `59d2a19` to `9ac0e55` | `Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock` |
| Pane hub | `PaneHub`: one tokio task per pane link, a mutex inbox with a byte cap and a `Notify` for room, a `mpsc(64)` command queue, a `watch` for the size, a separate detach token so a `Detach` never waits behind a full queue, a registry of the Codex links for the leave rule, a live-task count | `crates/jackin-tui/src/panes.rs` |
| Links | `HubLinks: LinkSource` (`open`, `restart`) and `HubLink: PaneLink`. A Codex pane runs `Session::attach` (`restart`: `Session::restart_agent` first, as `jackin attach --restart` does); a shell pane runs `Session::open_shell`. `drain` splits an output event at the budget and never reorders | `panes.rs` |
| Backend | `spawn_pane` builds a baseline `TerminalPane` over the hub; `initial_panes` returns the Codex pane of a running instance; `spawnable` refuses a second Codex pane; `pane_timing` reads `REPAINT_NUDGE`; `due` detaches the Codex panes of an instance whose daemon is no longer attached | `backend.rs` |
| Console | `ConsoleDeps.clipboard`, `ConsoleLimits.{pane_inbox_bytes, supervisor_poll}`, `AppOptions.panes = PanePolicy::default()`, `Console::{pane_tasks, close_panes}`; `Inputs::pane_target` resolves an instance to its session and sandbox | `console.rs`, `effects.rs` |
| Native sink | `ClipboardWriter` in `jackin-session` writes to `pbcopy`, `wl-copy`, `xclip` or `xsel` on its own thread and answers within 500 ms; `HostClipboard` in `jackin-tui` implements the baseline `NativeClipboard` over it | `jackin-session/src/clipboard_writer.rs`, `jackin-tui/src/clipboard.rs` |
| P-12 | `AttachedTerminal::reply` (sends only while the supervisor reads `running`), `AttachedTerminal::supervisor`, `REPAINT_NUDGE` is public | `jackin-session/src/attach.rs` |
| Testkit | TK-10 `SandboxHandle::undelivered_main_output`, TK-11 `SandboxHandle::close_main_streams`, `RecordingClipboard`, `ConsoleRig::{clipboard, wait_for_text, pump}`, builder `clipboard` and `pane_inbox_bytes` | `jackin-testkit` |
| Deleted | `UnbuiltPane` (the `Unbuilt::Pane` sentence is no longer used); `ratatui` moved to the dev-dependencies of `jackin-tui` | `unbuilt.rs`, `Cargo.toml` |

`jackin attach` and `jackin shell` keep the CLI filter (`ClipboardGuard`); nothing in `crates/jackin` changed. Ctrl-D is never sent to Codex: the baseline `ForwardPolicy` drops `0x04` and `0x11` in every key and paste, J5 adds no second filter, and `ctrl_d_never_reaches_codex_and_ctrl_b_ctrl_b_sends_the_prefix` reads the raw log of the real transport (red under `BL1`).

### J5: decisions the brief did not state, and deviations

1. **Base.** The branch was at `5af16cf6c`, before the J2 merge. J2 changes the same files as BG2 (`effects.rs`, `persist.rs`, `project.rs`), so adopting BG2 a second time would only conflict. The branch is fast-forwarded to `feature/openshell-native` (`bfe2f93ea`). That tree does not compile: J2 calls `plan_account_edits(&Config, edits)` and the old apply signatures, and P10.2 changed both. I picked J3's fix `8ecd57b69` (`dff9486cb`, same patch, so a later merge sees it once) instead of writing a second one.
2. **P-12 and TK-10 were not in J0a.** The J0a record is the v6 content. `AttachedTerminal::reply` and `undelivered_main_output` are v7.1 J0a items that J5 needs, so they are built here, with TK-11 (a stream that closes after the attach without an exit), which the reviewed plan lists as a missing knob (N18).
3. **The Capsule leaves by `Go::Detach`, which sets `Daemon.attached_by = None`.** The baseline gives the backend no call for it. `Backend::due` runs on every world tick and detaches the Codex links of every daemon with `attached_by == None` (UX rule: the Codex panes keep their attachment while the Capsule is open, and `Ctrl+B d` leaves it). Shells are not registered, so they stay. A detached pane shows the baseline `Detached from Codex` note and Enter reattaches. Quit drops the links, which detaches by drop.
4. **A Codex exit has no transport event.** `jackin-start` only prints a line (D09). The pane task polls `AttachedTerminal::supervisor` every `ConsoleLimits.supervisor_poll` and turns `exited <attempt> <code>` into `LinkEvent::Exited(Some(code))`; the pane then drops the link, which detaches the main stream. The test rig polls every 20 ms; the production value is chosen at J6.
5. **Input and replies.** Keys go through `AttachedTerminal::input` (it keeps its reply splitter, so a pasted answer-looking sequence is dropped while Codex is not running); replies go through `reply`; a shell uses `TerminalSession::input` for both. The reason of a failed open is `plain(error_chain(error))` of the typed error; nothing else is worded in jackin.
6. **The native sink is the host tools (O-11), not a crate.** `arboard` needs `clipboard-win` (BSL-1.0, not in `deny.toml`) and the `objc2` stack, and its write cannot be tested without touching the real clipboard. The tools are tested with shell scripts on a `PATH` of their own. The program names live in `jackin-session` because the `screen` step (C2) rejects every string literal in `jackin-tui`. The write waits for the tool on its own thread, so it never sleeps on the render thread, and it answers `false` when no tool took the text in 500 ms; the status then says `Copy requested`. A headless Linux host has no tool and says the same.
7. **Clipboard policy.** The decision is the baseline `decide` (shell never, read never, agent pane, focused, gesture within 5,000 ms, 100,000 decoded bytes); J5 passes `PanePolicy::default()` (`AgentOnGesture`) through `AppOptions.panes` and the sink through `ConsoleDeps.clipboard`. `ClipboardGuard` of the CLI is untouched.
8. **Tests were written after the code, and no independent reviewer saw the plan or the red runs** (no subagent tool). `test-reviewer` must review every new test below. The named breaks were run by `j5-red.py` (one edit set per break, `git checkout` after, the same filter in the next run); the suite is green on the final tree (gate below).
9. **Sizes differ from the plan.** `flood_is_bounded_and_lossless` sends 2 MiB with a 128 KiB inbox (plan: 4 MiB, 256 KiB) to keep the debug run near 7 s, and asserts more than 512 KiB undelivered, not 3 MiB, because the fake gives each attachment a channel of 256 events (1 MiB at 4 KiB). Lossless order is proved by the two unit tests of `Inbox`, not by a screen.
10. **Test bodies are `Box::pin`ned.** The first runs overflowed the 2 MiB stack of a test thread in debug builds (the async state of a body with a `Capsule` is larger than that). The shell rows end a command with `ctrl+j`, because the fake shell has no PTY to turn Enter into a newline.
11. **`console_fixture_args` changed** (a forced edit): the baseline scenario `capsule-terminal` also lands on the Capsule route.
12. **The scratchpad file `red.py` was overwritten** by my first harness (it was a shared scratchpad). My harnesses are `j5-red.py`, `j5-blred.py`, `j5-gate.sh` there.

### J5: baseline gaps

1. **No wake for a pane task.** There is no `Msg::PaneWake` (B8b decision 13), so a pane's output waits for the Capsule tick (80 ms). The plan row `wake_is_coalesced` cannot be written.
2. **`Backend::spawnable` needs a typed reason.** `Fixed` and `PaneWords` have no word for "a Codex pane is already open", and jackin may not write one. J5 returns an empty `Reason`: the picker row is disabled with no detail. Ask for `Fixed.codex_open` (or a `PaneWords` entry).
3. **No call when the Capsule is left.** J5 reads `Daemon.attached_by`. A `Backend::left(instance)` call would remove the dependence on a public field.
4. **Design 6.11 state 7 names `jackin doctor`; no typed value carries it.** The note shows the error text only.
5. **`ConsoleRig` has no wheel or drag.** The rows that need them (scrollback, selection and copy, the alternate-screen wheel, mouse mode, `y` after a drag) have no jackin code to break and are not written. The `Title` row of the info dialog is baseline-only as well.
6. **Not written for other reasons.** `refresh_keeps_open_panes` (T53): `Snapshot` cannot carry daemons, so no jackin edit can break it. `inspect_changes_lists_the_sandbox_diff`: `Effect::ReadChanges` is still answered by `unbuilt::fails`.

### J5: new tests, the named break of each, red and green

jackin-tui `crates/jackin-tui/tests/console/panes.rs` (28 rows, one binary `console`) and `crates/jackin-tui/src/panes.rs` (2 unit rows of `Inbox`); jackin-session `tests/attach/replies.rs` (2 rows) and `tests/clipboard_writer/main.rs` (4 rows); jackin-testkit `tests/gateway/witnesses.rs` (2 rows).

| Test | Plan row | Break |
|---|---|---|
| `the_pane_attaches_once_at_its_first_size_and_follows_the_layout` | T43 `first_size_is_the_only_size`, T48 | H1, H2, H3 |

`a_cut_escape_sequence_at_the_start_of_the_replay_never_shows` has no named break that turns it red alone: the hidden grid during the replay window and the repaint are the baseline's, and the transport nudge is `S4`. It passes on the baseline `TerminalPane` as built and stays as a guard for the J side of P0.6 check 3 (the cut replay plus the fake nudge); a reviewer may prefer to delete it.

Method: `j5-red.py` applies the edit set of a break to the committed tree, runs `nextest` with the filter of the rows (`-p jackin-tui` `test(/panes::/)`, the session binary, or the testkit rows), and runs `git checkout` on the edited files before the next break. `BL1` to `BL5` edit a clone of the baseline at `9ac0e55` and build `jackin-tui` against it with a `[patch]` in the `.cargo/config.toml` of a copy of the worktree (nothing under `~/.cargo` and no manifest of the repository changed). A break that did not compile (unused item under the workspace `deny`) was redone as a compiling edit; the rows below are the compiling ones. The suite had 28 `panes::` rows for the runs before `H27`, and 30 after the two rows `a_second_client_...` and `a_paste_...`; the line numbers are those of the test file at the time of the run. `S` is `jackin-session`, `H` is `jackin-tui`, `T` is `jackin-testkit`, `C` is the clipboard writer, `BL` is the baseline.

Every row above is red with a disagreement, not a compile error. Flakes found along the way: `H17` and `H18` turned the first clipboard row red on a loaded machine (`pump(6)` after a write), the `Inside` case of the window row depended on the simulated time that the waits add, and the bell row waited a fixed number of ticks. The rows now wait for a marker the pane has fed or for the signal itself. After each fix the 28 or 30 rows ran green three times in a row, and twice under 12 busy loops.

### J5: not proven

- The real `pbcopy`, `wl-copy`, `xclip` and `xsel` were not run (a run would overwrite the operator's clipboard); only shell scripts stand in for them. A tool that hangs leaves its worker thread behind.
- The production value of `supervisor_poll` and the live latency of the 80 ms tick (R-7) are for J6 and P9.5.
- `Reason` for a refused second Codex pane is empty (baseline gap 2).
- The takeover of the input lease by a pane (state 4 to state 3) is proved with the fake's lease rules, not with OpenShell.

### J5: API.md and design notes for the orchestrator

- `jackin-session`: `AttachedTerminal::reply`, `AttachedTerminal::supervisor`, `REPAINT_NUDGE` (public), `ClipboardWriter`, `ClipboardTool`, `HostOs`.
- `jackin-tui`: `ConsoleDeps.clipboard`, `ConsoleLimits.pane_inbox_bytes`, `ConsoleLimits.supervisor_poll`, `Console::pane_tasks`, `Console::close_panes`, `HostClipboard::{detect, over}`.
- `jackin-testkit`: `SandboxHandle::undelivered_main_output`, `SandboxHandle::close_main_streams`, `RecordingClipboard`, `ConsoleRig::{clipboard, wait_for_text, pump}`.
- D28: the native sink is the host tools; the Capsule leave rule is read from `Daemon.attached_by`; a Codex exit is read from the supervisor state by the pane task.

## J5 merge: J2 to J4 into P9.3 J5

Task P9.3, jackin lane. Merge of `origin/feature/openshell-native` (`e2bc476eb`, J2 persistence, J3 sources, 1Password and usage, J4 launch and cockpit on BG3) into `wt/P9.3-J5` (`8db9cfde6`). Merge base `bfe2f93ea`. The pin is `6870a19082aa00e7719120c067d34ef83ab80d6a` on both D27 lines in `Cargo.toml` and on the two baseline sources in `Cargo.lock`; it contains J5's `9ac0e55`. The difference between `9ac0e55` and `6870a19` is B8b's batch fix round and BG3 (the launch API J4 already adopted, `PaneWords`); the pane code J5 uses (`TerminalPane`, `LinkSource::restart`, `PaneLink`, `PasteOutcome`) needed no change, and J5 implements no `PaneBody`, so the `note(&PaneWords)` change did not reach it.

| File | Resolution |
|---|---|
| `Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock` | Origin's `6870a19` on both D27 lines and both lock sources. |
| `crates/jackin-tui/Cargo.toml` | Origin's `jackin-image`, `jackin-usage` and `serde_json` (dev); J5's `ratatui` stays a dev-dependency. |
| `dff9486cb` and `8ecd57b69` | One patch, one copy: `sessions.rs` and `tests/console/accounts.rs` equal origin's, and `effects.rs` differs from origin only by J5's `pane_target`. |
| `unbuilt.rs` | `fails` with the `Restore` and `Prewarm` arms only. J5's `ReadChanges` arm is gone because J3 built `Effect::ReadChanges`; J4 and J3 removed the launch, op and probe stubs; J5 removed `UnbuiltPane`. |
| `backend.rs` | Origin's `settle` (J2 persistence, J3 checks, op and usage), `HostFiles`, `launch::start`, `pub(crate)` `inputs` and `bridge`; J5's `PaneHub`, `spawn_pane` over `TerminalPane`, `initial_panes`, `spawnable`, `pane_timing` and `due`. |
| `observe.rs` | Origin's `Inputs` and `InputsParts` fields (`usage`, `usage_gate`, `op`, `op_cli`, `sources`, `env`). J5 adds none. |
| `bridge.rs` | Merged without a conflict: origin's `Settled` spawn, `handle`, `detach` and `send`. |
| `console.rs` | `ConsoleDeps` has origin's `usage` and J5's `clipboard`; `ConsoleLimits` has J5's `pane_inbox_bytes` and `supervisor_poll`; the `PaneHub` is built from the shared `Inputs`; the clipboard sink is `ClipboardMode::Host(clipboard)`. |
| `testkit/src/console.rs` | Origin's `usage` build and `UsageDeps`; J5's `RecordingClipboard`, `pane_inbox_bytes`, `supervisor_poll` and `clipboard` fields in the rig. |
| `tests/console/main.rs` | Modules of both sides: `onepassword`, `panes`, `probes`, `sources`, `usage_windows`. |
| `tests/fixture_mode.rs` | One `console_fixture_args` edit: J4 and J5 changed the line the same way, so the merged file allows `CapsuleMulti` and `CapsuleTerminal` once. |
| `P9.3.md` | Sections in time order: J2 merge, J4, J3, J4 on BG3, J4 merge, J5, this section. |

J5's note that `ReadChanges` is "still answered by `unbuilt::fails`" (J5 baseline gap 6) is out of date after this merge; the row `inspect_changes_lists_the_sandbox_diff` is J3's and is built. The J5 section keeps its original text.

`xtask baseline-branch` prints `ahead`, not `identical`: the baseline `feature/openshell-native` has moved three commits past `6870a19` (`a28059a`, `8497147`, `07f76db`, the BG4 typed reasons), which the JBG4 task adopts. The pin is unchanged here (`gh api` compare: `ahead_by=3`, `behind_by=0`).

## JBG4: baseline BG4 and BG5, J3 account texts typed, and the J2 login-folder bug

Task P9.3, jackin lane. Branch `wt/P9.3-JBG4`, base `e2bc476eb` (J1 to J4 on `6870a19`). Both D27 lines in `Cargo.toml` and both baseline sources in `Cargo.lock` move to `912b0a8b7bb35c78fe4e643008fcfe7c68073198` (BG5 on top of BG4 `07f76db`); `xtask baseline-branch` prints `912b0a8b7bb35c78fe4e643008fcfe7c68073198 identical`. The brief first pinned `07f76db` (commit 1); the coordinator then moved the pin to `912b0a8` and asked for `origin/feature/openshell-native` (J5, `6eac91488`) to be merged first. The merge was clean. The baseline sections BG4 and BG5 of `docs/P9.3-red-green.md` were read with `git show`; the baseline was only read.

### JBG4: what changed

| Gap | Before | Now | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| BG4 1 | `RecoverableIssue.message`, `CheckRow` text and `Refresh.outcome` were strings from `error_chain`, `OpError::message()`, `UnavailableReason` display and `Freshness::label()` | every `SourceFault` is an `IssueReason` (`EnvInvalid`, `EnvMissing`, `EnvEmpty`, `FileMissing`, `FileNotRegular`, `FolderMissing`, `FolderUnreadable { path, detail }`, `LoginMissing`, `LoginInvalid`, `BadReference`, `OnePassword(op_error)`); every `SourceFound` a `Found` (`EnvSet`, `FolderFound`, `Login`, `ReferenceResolved`); every `UnavailableReason` a `UsageReason` (`RateLimited.retry_in_secs = retry_at - now`); `Freshness` a `RefreshOutcome::{Current, Stale, Failed}`; a skipped row is `Skipped(IssueReason::Skipped)`; the quota row is `Found::Level(QuotaReadable)` | `checks.rs`, `usage.rs` |
| BG4 2 | the form listed four providers | `Surfaces::openshell` already carries `AccountForm::openshell()` (14 providers); no jackin code changed. The T30 provider-list test is built | `tests/console/sources.rs` |
| BG4 3 | `fields: 0`, `access: ReadWrite` | `fields: None`; `access: Some(Denied)` when `op item list` is refused, `None` otherwise | `onepassword.rs` |
| BG4 4 | `accounts()` returned an empty slice | returns the accounts `LoadOp(Accounts)` cached in the shared `OpSnapshot` | `onepassword.rs` |
| BG4 5 | `Session::changes` lost `omitted` and the unread flag | `ChangeSet.omitted` and `ChangeSet.unread` (`UnreadFile { path, status }`); an unread file is listed in `unread` and not in `files` (the widget chains the two) | `changes.rs` |
| BG5 1 | none | every `Inbox::push` calls a wake that sends `Msg::PaneWake { instance, pane }` through the `Bridge`; `PaneHub::links(pane)` carries the pane id from `PaneSpawn.id` | `panes.rs`, `backend.rs`, `console.rs` |
| BG5 2 | `Err(Reason::new(""))` | `Err(PaneRefusal::MainProcessOpen { agent })` for a second agent pane | `backend.rs` |
| BG5 3 | `due` read `Daemon.attached_by` and called `leave` | `capsule_left` calls `leave`; the `due` override is deleted | `backend.rs` |
| BG5 4 | `open`, `restart` and the end events carried strings | `LinkFault::new(error_chain)`; `.then(FailNext::RunDoctor)` when the fault is a gateway connection fault (`GatewayError::{Unreachable, SandboxUnreachable, Tls, Unauthenticated}` inside `AttachError::Gateway` or `RestartError::Gateway`) or the home gateway is down (`pane_target`, `Access::Down`) | `panes.rs`, `effects.rs` |
| J2 bug | `persist::credential` stored a non-Codex local login folder as `File { path: <directory> }`; `jackin-usage` read the directory as a text file | the folder resolves to the provider's login file (`Provider::login_file` in `jackin-core`), checked with `stat_following` on the bridge; `Folder` in `persist.rs` is now `Chatgpt { home, account }` or `Login { file }`; a folder without that file, or a provider without a login file, is refused with `Rejection::NotAFolder` | `persist.rs`, `effects.rs`, `jackin-core/src/provider.rs` |

### JBG4: decisions the brief did not state, and deviations

1. **`jackin-core` has no folder kind.** `CredentialSource` is `Env`, `OnePassword`, `File` and `CodexLogin`. DESIGN D26 gives a non-Codex account `env:`, `op://` or `file:PATH` (a credential file), so `File` is the right kind and its path must be the login file, not the directory. I added `Provider::login_file()` with the names the old discovery used (`470fc5d6:crates/core/jackin-config/src/accounts/discovery.rs:310`): `.credentials.json` (Claude), `oauth_creds.json` (Gemini), `credentials/kimi-code.json` (Kimi), `secrets.json` (Amp), `auth.json` (Codex, Cursor, Grok, Muse, OpenCode); Antigravity, Hermes, MiniMax, OpenRouter and Z.AI have none, so a folder is refused for them. The names are from the old code, not from a recorded transcript of each agent. The brief's "find the folder kind" had no target.
2. **The form check is unchanged.** A readable directory still passes `CheckSource` for a non-Codex provider (J3 decision 8); the refusal comes at save. A folder without the login file therefore validates and then fails to save with "`{folder}` is not a folder jackin can use". Moving the check onto the login file needs a `SourceDesk` API change (the `login: bool` of `Source::Folder`), left out.
3. **A saved login file is shown as a local folder with the file path**, because `project::source` projects `CredentialSource::File` as `LocalFolder` (J2). The path round-trips and an unchanged source is never re-resolved.
4. **`SourceFault::NotChatgpt` is `IssueReason::LoginInvalid`** as the gap table says, so an API-key `auth.json` reads "auth.json is not valid JSON". The issue code stays `FolderWrongProvider`. The text is not true for a valid API-key login.
5. **`FolderUnreadable.detail` is `error_chain(fault)`** for `Unreadable`, `Login` and `Fs`, the host's text; for `Login` and `Fs` the path is the path typed in the form.
6. **The sentinel `QuotaWindow.note` is `Vocabulary::openshell().reason(..)`**, the way `persist.rs` words a rejection. The live `Vocabulary` is not held by `Inputs`.
7. **`OpSnapshot.accounts()` is filled when the picker lists accounts** and is not refreshed until it lists them again. After one listing with exactly one account the picker starts at its vaults. A second account added later is not seen until the picker is opened with no cached account.
8. **`AUTH_FILE` of `jackin-session/src/source.rs` is public** (it was private) so `checks.rs` does not repeat the literal.
9. **`authorization_and_a_denied_vault_each_show_their_text` is `guard(Box::pin(..))`**: after the J5 merge its future overflowed the test thread's stack (`SIGABRT`, "stack overflow"). The J5 pane tests already box theirs. Two of my tests were split for the same reason.
10. **The pre-commit hook runs clippy** and rejected the first form of the T30 test (`clippy::excessive_nesting`); the test was restructured, not allowed.
11. **No independent reviewer.** I had no subagent tool. `test-reviewer` must review the plan and the red runs below.

## J6: `jackin` opens the console, and `xtask product-check`

Task P9.3, jackin lane, step J6 of `/private/tmp/claude-501/plans/P9.3-design-v7.1.md` (section 7 J6 row, 3.4, 3.7.5 items 3 and 4, G4) with design v6 section 12 and test rows T1 and the artifact half of T34 (`/private/tmp/claude-501/plans/P9.3-test-plan-v6.md`, `P9.3-test-plan-v7.1-delta.md`, amendments N4 of `P9.3-test-plan-v7.1-review.md`). Branch `wt/P9.3-J6`, base `6eac91488` (J1 to J5 merged). The baseline pin is unchanged (`6870a19082aa00e7719120c067d34ef83ab80d6a`); no baseline file was touched. JBG4 (BG4) owns `checks.rs`, `onepassword.rs`, `usage.rs`, `persist.rs`, `op.rs`, `source.rs` and `launch.rs` in `crates/jackin-tui`; none of them is touched here.

### J6: what changed

| Item | Change | Where |
|---|---|---|
| Entry | `run` with no command and a terminal calls `commands::console::run`; with no terminal (stdin or stdout is not a terminal, decided by `SystemTerminal::open`) it prints the help and exits 0, as before | `crates/jackin/src/lib.rs` |
| Console wiring | loads the config (an error stops before the host runs), picks the default gateway, connects every gateway concurrently, builds `ConsoleDeps` from `Deps` and calls `Deps.console`; `Quit` is `ExitClass::Ok`, a `ConsoleError` is `CommandError::Console` (`Failure`, message on stderr, stdout empty) | `crates/jackin/src/commands/console.rs` |
| `Deps.console` | `Arc<dyn ConsoleHost>`; `main` passes `TerminalConsole` | `crates/jackin/src/app.rs`, `main.rs` |
| `ConsoleHost`, `run_console` | `#[async_trait] trait ConsoleHost { async fn run(&self, ConsoleDeps) -> Result<ConsoleExit, ConsoleError> }`, the real host `TerminalConsole`, `run_console(deps)` = `run_console_with(deps, junie_tui::shell::run)`. `run_console_with` builds the console and runs the terminal loop on one `spawn_blocking` thread, drops the app, waits up to 3 s for the pane tasks, then returns `Quit`. A terminal error is `ConsoleError::Terminal`; a panic in the loop is the new `ConsoleError::Panicked` | `crates/jackin-tui/src/console.rs` |
| `system_theme` | `Theme::for_level(ColorLevel::detect())`, so `jackin` need not name a `junie-tui` type and `NO_COLOR` is honoured by the baseline | `crates/jackin-tui/src/console.rs` |
| `SessionSet::connect` | the J1b item 2: connects every configured gateway at once; each attempt is cut at the deadline (`Failed(Unreachable, "no answer within 5s")`); a refusal is `Failed(error)` | `crates/jackin-tui/src/sessions.rs`, `fleet.rs` (`within`, `Waited`, `TimedOut` are `pub(crate)`) |
| Production limits | `connect_deadline` 5 s, `supervisor_poll` 250 ms, `work_reads` 4, `pane_inbox_bytes` 1 MiB (`console_limits()`); the pane close wait is the constant `CLOSE_DEADLINE` of 3 s | `crates/jackin/src/system.rs`, `crates/jackin-tui/src/console.rs` |
| Terminal | `SystemTerminal` starts its stdin reader thread on the first `read_line` or `next_input`, not in `open`. The console reads the terminal through crossterm, and an eager reader thread would take its keys | `crates/jackin/src/system.rs` |
| Session factory | `App::session_maker()` returns the closure that builds a `Session` over a connected gateway; `session_of` uses it; `GatewaySessions` uses it for the console | `crates/jackin/src/app.rs` |
| `xtask product-check` | `ProductCheck<R>` (a `Check`, step `DemoGate`): runs the whole `demo-gate` check first (static R3, feature graph, rlib `nm` and `strings`), stops there on a violation, otherwise runs `cargo build --release -p jackin --message-format=json`, takes the executable from the `compiler-artifact` record of the `bin` target `jackin`, reads the file and fails on any of the five canaries. Exit 0 or 1 | `crates/xtask/src/checks/demo_gate.rs`, `crates/xtask/src/main.rs`, `COMMITS.md` |
| Tests deleted | `a_bare_invocation_prints_help_on_a_terminal_too` (the "help fallback at `help.rs:98`" of the plan is this test; the production help arm stays for the no-terminal case) | `crates/jackin/tests/cli/help.rs` |
| Testkit | `PtyCommand`/`PtyRun` (a `portable-pty` driver that answers the device-attributes query like a terminal), `ConsoleRigBuilder::deps()` | `crates/jackin-testkit/src/pty.rs`, `console.rs` |
| Manifests | `portable-pty = "0.9.0"` moves to `[workspace.dependencies]` (`cargo deny` `workspace-duplicate`); `jackin-testkit` and `jackin-xtask` use `workspace = true` | `Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock` |

### J6: decisions the design did not state, and deviations

1. **Production values.** `ConsoleLimits.connect_deadline` is 5 s. It is also the J1b doctor deadline: the start run, the `r` run and the 60 s timer all read `connect_deadline`, so the doctor has no separate value. `supervisor_poll` is 250 ms (J5 left it for J6). `work_reads` 4 and `pane_inbox_bytes` 1 MiB are the testkit values and the design default. Nothing in DESIGN names a different value. Measure at P9.5 (R-7, R-10).
2. **`ConsoleHost::run` and `run_console` are async.** The design shows sync signatures; `build_console` is async since J1 (decision 1). `run_console_with(deps, terminal)` is the one seam: the terminal loop is a closure, so the wrapper (blocking thread, error mapping, pane close) is tested without a real terminal. `run_console` passes `junie_tui::shell::run`.
3. **The console is built on the blocking thread.** `jackin_console::app::App` is not `Send`, so it cannot be built on the runtime thread and moved. `run_console_with` calls `Handle::block_on(build_console(deps))` inside `spawn_blocking`; the effect and pane tasks still run on the main current-thread runtime, which the main thread polls while it awaits the join handle.
4. **Panic.** A panic in the loop gives `ConsoleError::Panicked` (exit `Failure`). The terminal is restored by the baseline: `TerminalSession::enter_with` installs a panic hook that runs `restore_terminal` and `Restoration` restores on drop. J6 adds nothing to it. A real panic in the real loop is not tested (see "not proven").
5. **No default gateway.** With no gateway configured, or several and no default, `SessionSet` still needs a default name. `default_gateway` uses the placeholder `none` (not in the config), every configured gateway is still connected and listed, and a launch fails with the existing `UnknownGateway(none)` text. An explicit `--gateway` that is not in the config stops before the console (`ConfigError::UnknownGateway`, class `Failure`). The right fix is an `Option<GatewayName>` in `SessionSet` (or the resolve error) and a typed text in `launch.rs`; `launch.rs` belongs to JBG4, so this waits for its merge.
6. **T1 as built.** The plan's `ScriptedConsoleHost` runs a key script and checks the first frame text. The jackin-crate host builds the console from the `ConsoleDeps` it gets and records what it saw (default gateway, which gateways connected, the number of workspaces in the built `World`, `cwd`, `home`, the two limits). The first screen of the real binary is checked by the PTY test. The T1 sentence "a config with zero gateways: the doctor chrome shows failing" is not met: J1b baseline gap 3 (a missing gateway gives `Skipped` rows and a healthy chrome) still stands. The test checks that the console starts.
7. **PTY test and the intro.** With no running sandbox the baseline plays the intro first, and `intro_guard` swallows the first keys. The PTY test sets `JACKIN_NO_MOTION=1` (the reduced intro ends by itself after about 1.5 s), waits for the manager (`alpha`, `beta`), sends `q`, and has the shell print `stty -g` before and after the run. `RESTORED:0` means the exit code was 0 and the terminal settings are byte-equal. The PTY driver answers `CSI c` with `CSI ? 62 c`, as a terminal does; without the answer crossterm waits 2 s at start.
8. **Two PTY tests for the no-terminal half**, because `SystemTerminal::open` tests both ends: `jackin > file` (stdout not a terminal, stdin the PTY) and `jackin < /dev/null` (stdin not a terminal, stdout the PTY). Both print the help, exit 0 and never enter the alternate screen.
9. **The artifact scan reads the file; it does not run `strings`.** On macOS `strings -a` scans object sections only, and a canary written after the last section was not found (probe in this run, 0 hits on a planted string). GNU `strings -` means stdin. `ProductCheck` reads the executable bytes and searches the lossy text for the five canaries. The rlib scan of J1a still uses `strings -a`; its objects keep strings in sections, so the macOS rule does not hide a real constant, but this is inferred, not measured.
10. **No `nm` on the artifact.** `[profile.release]` has `strip = "symbols"`. The symbol half of `product-check` is the rlib `nm` check of `demo-gate` on the debug `jackin-tui` build; the artifact half is the canary scan, which design 3.7.5 item 4 already calls weak.
11. **`product-check` stops after a `demo-gate` violation**, so a graph or symbol violation does not cost a release build. The test for it removes the scripted release reply, so a build that runs shows as an `unscripted command` violation.
12. **`product-check` is not in `xtask ci` and not in `CiStep`.** It is a subcommand, as `baseline-branch` is. Its violations use step `demo-gate`. `COMMITS.md` names it.
13. **SIGINT.** `main` still cancels on the first `Ctrl+C` signal. In raw mode `Ctrl+C` is a key for the console, so only an external `kill -INT` reaches the signal. The console does not read `AppEnv.cancel`; the first external SIGINT is ignored, and a second one makes `main` return 130 only after the loop ends, because the runtime waits for the blocking thread (read from `main.rs` and the tokio rules; not run). Left as is; the baseline loop has no external quit message.
14. **Tests were written before the code for xtask (`ProductCheck` stub), `run_console_with`/`SessionSet::connect` (stubs) and the jackin cli rows (stub `commands::console::run`); the reds are below.** The PTY rows and `PtyRun` were written after the implementation (a screen needs a console); their reds are breaks J1, J2, J3 and J8. I had no subagent tool, so no independent reviewer saw the plan or the red runs. `test-reviewer` is needed for every row below.

### Baseline gaps (go back to the baseline, never into jackin)

- No external quit message (`Remote` is `Msg` or `Reloaded`), so the console cannot honour a cancel token or a signal while the loop runs.
- `Fixed`/doctor gap 3 of J1b (a missing gateway shows a healthy chrome) blocks the T1 sentence about a failing chrome.
- In a probe at 60 columns by 20 rows `q` did not quit within 4 s while the loop kept polling (a stack sample showed it in `crossterm::event::poll`); at 90 by 26 `q` quits. Not investigated further.

### J6: new tests, the named break of each, red and green

Method: `j6-red.py` (task scratchpad) applies one edit per break to production code, asserts that the old text occurs once, runs `mise exec -- mbx +1.99.1 nextest run -p <crate> --no-fail-fast -E '<filter>'`, restores the file and runs the same filter again. A first draft of breaks X4, X5, X6, S1, J2, J5 and J7 did not compile (`unused` lints under the workspace `deny`) and was rewritten; X6 and H1 needed a second form (H1: the test now starts its sender task only after the closure runs, because the first form let the task run during `build_console`). The first lines below are the failing assertion or error.

- `console` (jackin cli): 10 tests run: 4 passed, 6 failed; `index out of bounds: the len is 0 but the index is 0`; `left: Ok` | `right: Failure`; `left: 0` | `right: 1`.

The real-tool run of `product-check` (no release build) is below under "Real run".

### Real run (not the release build)

`xtask product-check` was run with `CARGO` pointing at a shim that answers only `cargo build --release -p jackin --message-format=json` with the recorded debug `compiler-artifact` record and passes every other call to the real cargo. The feature graph, the debug `jackin-tui` build, `nm` and `strings` on the rlib, and the byte scan ran for real, on the debug binary.

- Clean: exit 0, stderr `xtask: product-check`.
- A copy of the debug binary with `org.jackin.workspace=payments-platform` appended, named by the shimmed record: exit 1, stderr `<path> holds the demo canary "payments-platform"` and `xtask: product-check reported 1 violations`.
- The five canaries are absent from `target/debug/jackin` (`strings -a | grep -c` printed 0 for each).

### J6: not proven

- The release build itself. The command for the main session:

  ```sh
  cd /Users/donbeave/Projects/jackin-project/wt/jackin-J6 && mise exec -- mbx +1.99.1 xtask product-check
  ```

  Exit 0 means the demo-gate checks passed and the release binary named by cargo holds none of the five canaries. It prints `xtask: product-check` first, builds `-p jackin` with `lto = "thin"` (several minutes), and prints each violation on stderr. Run it from the repository root.
- A panic in the real loop restoring the terminal. The baseline hook and `Restoration::drop` do it; `host::a_panic_in_the_loop_is_an_error_and_not_a_hang` proves only that the wrapper turns the panic into `ConsoleError::Panicked` and does not hang.
- The wait for pane tasks on quit (`panes.finished(CLOSE_DEADLINE)`): no test opens a pane and quits. J5 proves the hub itself.
- SIGWINCH: checked by hand with a Python PTY probe (a resize to 90x26 redraws the whole screen with `alpha`, and `q` then exits 0 and leaves the alternate screen). There is no test.
- Colour levels and `NO_COLOR`: `system_theme` calls the baseline `ColorLevel::detect`; no test.
- The cold start time of the console on a real gateway (R-10): the PTY test starts with no gateway.

### J6: API.md and design notes for the orchestrator

- `jackin-tui`: `ConsoleHost`, `TerminalConsole`, `run_console`, `run_console_with`, `system_theme`, `ConsoleError::Panicked`, `SessionSet::connect`.
- `jackin`: `Deps.console`; `pub(crate)` `console_limits()` and `home()`; `App::session_maker()`.
- `jackin-testkit`: `PtyCommand`, `PtyRun`, `ConsoleRigBuilder::deps()`.
- `jackin-xtask`: `ProductCheck`, subcommand `product-check`.
- DESIGN CLI surface row 1 is met. Design 12 "Initial route: the manager; Current directory is `Config::workspace_for(cwd)`" is done by the baseline manager from `World.cwd` (`apps/jackin/src/world.rs:749`, `screens/manager.rs:220`); J6 passes the real `cwd`. No jackin test drives a launch from the current directory in the PTY; the cwd reaches the host (`Run.cwd` is asserted).
- `decisions.tsv` (orchestrator): production limits (item 1), the async `ConsoleHost` (item 2), the placeholder gateway name (item 5), the byte scan of the artifact (item 9).
