## P9.4: the D27 identity gate

Task P9.4. Branch `wt/P9.4`, base `0330340ed` (J1 to J6 merged, baseline pinned at `912b0a8`). Design: `/private/tmp/claude-501/plans/P9.3-design-v7.1.md` lines 166, 331, 480, 803, 907 and 946 (A47, G16).

### P9.4: what changed

| Item | Change | Where |
|---|---|---|
| Gate | nextest target `identity` of `jackin-testkit`: it runs the `console-fixture` binary in a PTY (`tuiscotti-tui-test` `Session`, 120x40, the baseline environment, `--color truecolor` or `NO_COLOR`), drives it with the keys of each baseline journey, and compares the settled screen with the approved `jackin-preview` file by `tuiscotti::check::FrozenStore::check`, the call that the baseline `App::visual` makes (state gate: text, colours, attributes, cursor) | `crates/jackin-testkit/tests/identity/` (`app.rs` driver and compare, `steps.rs` shared steps, `manager.rs`, `fixed.rs`, `capsule.rs` journeys, `coverage.rs`, `baseline.rs`) |
| Approved files | not copied. `baseline.rs` runs `cargo metadata --offline --locked`, takes the manifest directory of the package `jackin-console` (the git checkout of the pinned rev) and reads `tests/functional/baselines` there. `FrozenStore` opens the baseline root read-only and writes evidence under `CARGO_TARGET_TMPDIR/identity` | `baseline.rs` |
| Dependencies | dev dependencies of `jackin-testkit`: `tuiscotti-tui-test =0.2.1` (the baseline's pin) and `tuiscotti` at git rev `d8d54155947f6303a41dfd6079b53c0abc16f29a` (the baseline's pin). `deny.toml`: `allow-git` gains `https://github.com/tailrocks/tuiscotti`; two `advisories.ignore` rows (`RUSTSEC-2026-0206` rustybuzz, `RUSTSEC-2026-0192` ttf-parser) that tuiscotti's own `deny.toml` also ignores | `Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock`, `crates/jackin-testkit/Cargo.toml`, `deny.toml` |
| `baseline` step | R6 requires the exact `allow-git` set; it now lists the tuiscotti URL as the fourth source, and the R4 source-table rule names it too | `crates/xtask/src/checks/baseline.rs`, tests in `crates/xtask/tests/gates/{baseline,workspace}.rs`, `crates/xtask/tests/repo_gates.rs` |
| `console-fixture` | unchanged | |

### P9.4: journeys compared and excluded

The baseline has 50 functional journeys for `apps/jackin` (`tests/functional/jackin.rs` 47, `terminal_panes.rs` 3) and 54 approved files. The gate ports the key sequence of the 41 journeys that call `visual` and compares all 54 files. Each port keeps every key, paste, click, hover, wheel, clock step and wait of the baseline journey up to its last capture, and drops the text assertions and the steps after the last capture. Nothing in the 54 comparisons is excluded.

Nine baseline journeys call no `visual`, so they have no approved file to compare with. They are listed in `WITHOUT_CAPTURE` and not driven:

| Journey | Why it is not compared |
|---|---|
| `jackin_cockpit_docker_names_key_and_egress_bypass` | text assertions only |
| `jackin_roles_add_role` | text assertions only |
| `jackin_fixed_dirty_exit_refuses_the_list_by_key_and_click` | text assertions only |
| `jackin_fixed_working_directory_refuses_the_picker_by_key_and_click` | text assertions only |
| `jackin_fixed_last_allowed_role_cannot_be_unchecked` | text assertions only |
| `jackin_fixed_last_allowed_role_refuses_a_click` | text assertions only |
| `jackin_fixed_env_form_refuses_a_credential_reference_and_notes_a_secret_name` | text assertions only |
| `jackin_fixed_workspace_env_keeps_the_role_scope_live_and_refuses_the_sources` | text assertions only |
| `jackin_launch_questions_end_the_launch_on_escape` | text assertions only |

No journey needs a sim-only effect that `console-fixture` lacks: the binary builds the same `app_for` world as `jackin-preview`.

Scenarios: 13 of the 17 are driven by a journey with an approved file (`first-use`, `returning`, `accounts-mixed`, `launch-failure`, `docker-setting`, `docker-no-engine`, `doctor`, `role-binding`, `launch-openshell`, `role-editor`, `openshell-fixed`, `launch-checks`, `capsule-terminal`). `launch-running`, `capsule-multi`, `outro-last` and `hard-cases` have no approved `jackin-preview` file at `912b0a8` (the baseline checks them in process only), so there is nothing to compare; `WITHOUT_APPROVED_FILE` lists them.

The gate checks its own coverage on a pin bump. Three tests in `coverage.rs` read the baseline journey sources and the approved directory from the pinned checkout: every baseline `#[test]` with a `visual` call has a port of the same name that compares the same files; every baseline test without one is in `WITHOUT_CAPTURE`; the approved directory and the compared files are the same set; every `Scenario::ALL` entry is driven or listed in `WITHOUT_APPROVED_FILE`.

### P9.4: decisions the plan did not state, and deviations

1. **`console-fixture` is not built through `build_console`.** PLAN P9.4 and design 9.2 say the gate builds the console through `build_console`. `build_console` projects live jackin data (config, fleet, gateway health) through `JackinBackend`, so it shows `platform-backend` and `sentinel` with OpenShell words and answers an effect with the `Unbuilt` text; the baseline fixture worlds (`payments-platform`, Claude Code, the simulated saves) cannot be produced from jackin data, and no `build_console` caller can pass a `World`. Design 4.2, 6.1 (G16) and A47 decide the same: the demo and the product both call `assemble`, and `console-fixture` holds only argument parsing and `app_for`. The gate therefore proves that the pinned `jackin-console`, built by jackin's workspace with the same dependencies and run by jackin's `console-fixture`, draws every approved `jackin-preview` file. It does not prove that `build_console` draws the demo, which cannot hold. Orchestrator: PLAN P9.4 step 1 and design 9.2 should say `console-fixture` (`app_for`), not `build_console`.
2. **The ported journeys are written in jackin.** The keys live in `manager.rs`, `fixed.rs` and `capsule.rs`; the approved files stay in the checkout. A baseline journey whose keys change on a pin bump is not followed automatically; `coverage.rs` fails when a visual or a journey appears, disappears or moves to another file.
3. **Timeouts.** Each wait has 20 s (the baseline: 10 s) because the machine is loaded. The comparison is unchanged.
4. **`cargo metadata` per test process.** nextest starts one process per test, so each journey runs `cargo metadata --offline --locked` once (about 1 to 2 s). The program is the literal `cargo` on `PATH`: the `containers` check rejects a computed program such as `env!("CARGO")`.
5. **`xtask baseline` R6** needed the tuiscotti URL (item above). It is outside "gate, binary, tests" but the dependency cannot exist without it.
6. **decisions.tsv (orchestrator):** the tuiscotti dev dependency and its two advisory ignores; deviation 1.

### P9.4: red and green

Method: `harness/red.py` (task scratchpad) applies one edit to a source file, asserts the old text occurs once, runs `mise exec -- mbx +1.99.1 nextest run -p <crate> --no-fail-fast -E '<filter>'`, restores the file and runs the same filter again. The colour red below was run by hand: the approved file `jackin_launch_reconnect.tui.json` was copied with the rest of the directory to the task scratchpad, the first `"fg":"#8a8a8a"` (cell column 0, row 0) became `"fg":"#8a8a8b"`, and `root()` in `baseline.rs` pointed at the copy for one run.

| `IDENT-journey-key-dropped` (`jackin_doctor_failure_and_rerun`: the four `down` keys after `g` removed) | red | `manager::jackin_doctor_failure_and_rerun` | `functional/jackin_doctor differs from the approved jackin-preview baseline: 286 changed cells` | `1 test run: 1 passed, 168 skipped` |

The first row changed the last hex digit of the first `"fg"` value, `#8a8a8a` to `#8a8a8b`, in the copy. `FrozenStore` named column 0, row 0, field `fg`, total 1. The red and green runs of that row are the P9.4 proof of PLAN: a one-cell colour change fails the gate, and the revert passes it. `a_one_cell_colour_change_fails_the_check` keeps that proof in the suite: it changes the same digit in the real approved bytes and expects `changed_cell_total == 1`, and the unchanged bytes to pass.

The 41 journeys passed on the first run against the unchanged files (`Summary [  25.056s] 44 tests run: 44 passed, 0 skipped`, before the colour test was added), and again in a second full run (`45 tests run: 45 passed`). No journey flaked in the runs of this task at a load average of about 80 to 100. `test-reviewer` has not seen these runs; the independent red and green record is still to be taken.

## J2-J4 fix round

Task P9.3, jackin lane. Branch `wt/P9.3-Jfix`, base `5656b2981` (J1 to J5, JBG4, baseline `912b0a8`). Input: the batch review `/private/tmp/claude-501/plans/P9.3-J2-J4-review.md` (MEDIUM 3 was fixed in JBG4). The coordinator added items 9 and 10 during the round. `origin/feature/openshell-native` did not hold J6, so the local branch `wt/P9.3-J6` (tip `eca5b1294`) was merged for item 10; the only conflict was the end of this file (both sides appended), kept in order JBG4, J6, this section.

### J2-J4 fix round: what changed

| Item | Before | Now | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 MEDIUM 1 | `role_edits` read the saved default role by the new name, so rename plus roles plus default failed with `role sentinel is not in the allowed roles` | the saved default is read by the old name and passed as a bool | `jackin-tui/src/persist.rs` |
| 2 MEDIUM 2 | a rename emitted `RemoveWorkspace` plus `AddWorkspace` of a copy read before `ConfigStore::edit` | `ConfigEdit::RenameWorkspace { from, to }` moves the entry under the store lock; missing `from` and taken `to` are the existing missing and duplicate errors | `jackin-core/src/config.rs`, `config/edit.rs`, `persist.rs` |
| 3 LOW | `op item get <item>` took a dash-leading item, vault or account as a flag | the item follows `--` (after `--format json`); a dash-leading account, vault or item is refused with `OpFault::FlagLikeName`; a reference with a dash-leading segment is refused as `SourceFault::Reference` (UI: bad reference) before `op` runs | `jackin-session/src/op.rs`, `source.rs`, `jackin-tui/src/onepassword.rs` |
| 4 LOW | `tail_of` showed a whole short value; `OpSnapshot` derived `Debug` | no tail for a value of 8 characters or fewer; `OpSnapshot` has a manual `Debug` with counts only | `op.rs`, `jackin-tui/src/onepassword.rs` |
| 5 LOW | auth markers were tested before the "isn't a ..." markers, and `op` echoes the name | the not-found markers are tested first | `op.rs` |
| 6 LOW | a panic in a bridge task sent no reply; a panic in `observe()` left `refreshing` set | `Bridge::spawn(failure, work)` awaits the `JoinHandle` and sends the effect's failure reply (`crashed::reply`, exhaustive over `Effect`); `Bridge::detach(work, failed)` runs the launch's failure announcement (the failed stage is the last one started); `refreshing` is released by a drop guard | `bridge.rs`, `crashed.rs`, `backend.rs`, `launch.rs` |
| 7 LOW | the current directory failed to launch when its basename was not a workspace name | `WorkspaceName::sanitized` (lowercase, invalid runs to `-`, trimmed, at most 63, `workspace` when nothing is left) and `adhoc_workspace_name` (a clash with a saved workspace of another path gets `-<six hex of sha256(path)>`); a valid basename is unchanged; the directory stays the project | `jackin-core/src/ids.rs`, `jackin-session/src/workspace.rs`, `jackin-tui/src/launch.rs` |
| 8 JBG4 text | `NotChatgpt` was `LoginInvalid` ("auth.json is not valid JSON") | `LoginMissing { path, file: "ChatGPT login in auth.json" }` | `jackin-tui/src/checks.rs`, `jackin-session/src/source.rs` |
| 9 clipboard | the writer waited a fixed 500 ms of real time | `deadline` is a field of `ClipboardWriter` (default 2 s, `within(Duration)`); the tests pass 60 s; a hanging tool is not a write | `jackin-session/src/clipboard_writer.rs` |
| 10 gateway | J6 used the placeholder gateway `none` | `SessionSet` holds `Option<GatewayName>` (`default_name() -> Option<&GatewayName>`, `Home::NotChosen`, `Access::NotChosen` for none); `run_console` passes `None` for no gateway or several without a default; a launch with none fails at the first stage with the `resolve_gateway` text and `NextStep::RunDoctor` before any provider, sandbox or image | `jackin-tui/src/sessions.rs`, `observe.rs`, `launch.rs`, `jackin/src/commands/console.rs`, `jackin-testkit/src/console.rs` |

### J2-J4 fix round: decisions the brief did not state, and deviations

1. **Gap in BG4 (item 8).** `IssueReason` has no reason that says a login is not a ChatGPT login (`LoginInvalid` is "not valid JSON", `WrongProvider` is another provider's profile). `LoginMissing` is the closest true one: "No credential found: {path} has no ChatGPT login in auth.json" and the check row "Run the agent login first". Its `file` field carries a description, not a file name. The issue code stays `FolderWrongProvider`. A baseline reason such as `LoginNotChatgpt { file }` would be the exact fix.
2. **Item 3 refuses more than the item.** The brief says to refuse a dash-leading item, vault or account, so all three are refused although `--vault <value>` consumes its value. The text of the refusal in the console is only reachable for a typed reference, which `field_path` already turns into "not a reference". `FlagLikeName` maps to `OpError::NotAvailable` in the picker, which cannot send such a name (it passes ids).
3. **Item 5 orders, it does not strip.** Removing the echoed names from stderr breaks markers when a name is short (a vault named `e`). Testing the not-found markers first is enough; a name that contains `isn't an item` itself would still be misread.
4. **Item 6 sends no reload.** The failure reply of an effect that panicked is not followed by `Remote::Reloaded`, because rebuilding the snapshot is more work that can panic again. The editor keeps what the operator typed. The panic hook still prints its message.
5. **Item 6, launch.** The panic closure of a launch reports the last stage that sent `Running` (`Run.stage`), and refreshes the roster. A sandbox the launch half-created is not recorded (`instance: None`).
6. **Item 9, hanging tool.** The writer thread stays blocked in `child.wait()` for a tool that never exits; the write is reported as failed at the deadline, but the child and the thread live on. Killing the child needs the child handle outside the worker thread.
7. **Item 10, the test of the none case** uses a new `ConsoleRigBuilder::unchosen()`. The `Run` record of the J6 CLI test holds `Option<GatewayName>`; two CLI tests were added or changed (`a_config_without_a_gateway...` asserts `None`, `several_gateways_with_no_default...`). If the config resolves a gateway after the console started with none, the launch still says `NoGateway`.
8. **The 12-of-40 stress failure of item 9 was not reproduced here.** The fix removes the 500 ms real-time wait the tests depended on.
9. **No independent reviewer.** I had no subagent tool. `test-reviewer` must review the test plan and the red runs below.
10. **Left, not in the brief:** review findings 7 (second half: clear the `OpSnapshot` cache when the picker closes), 9 (a launch that reached Ready reported as failed when the record cannot be read), 11 (a window with only a remaining percentage), and the three questions. `OpFieldInfo` still derives `Debug` and prints its tail; only `OpSnapshot` was changed as asked.
